* `profiles` added to `supabase_realtime` so a flag change reaches an open tab immediately. Migration `20260922120000_profiles_realtime_and_activity_guard.sql`. Applied to the live DB via the Lovable agent.

* Dropped a planned `auth.admin.signOut(class_id)` call mid-build — that API takes a JWT, not a user id, so it would have silently failed on every archive. UI copy corrected to match.
* Tier design settled across 8 interview rounds. A tier is a template that sets the switches; switches remain enforcement. 12 switches, not 13. Tier 1 = all on, manual, indefinite, relabels to Tier 2 on any deviation. Tier 2 = individual toggles, persist as normal state, no snapshot restored. Tier 3 = floor (Classroom/Messages/Schedule) + assigned items only. Governing rule, stated twice by Joshua: access never changes by itself. Per item, never per feature.
* Plan written, pushed to branch `docs/student-access-tiers-plan` (`0d01fa9`), NOT merged — awaiting sign-off.

Session Handoff — LSAT U student access: fixed a broken revoke, added archive/delete, designed a three-tier access model
Where it started
Joshua asked how the platform's student access control currently works, wanting a better on/off toggle. Investigation found "Revoke All Access" did not actually revoke all access. That expanded into shipping two fixes, then a full `/grill-me` interview designing a three-tier access system, ending in a written implementation plan awaiting sign-off.
Decisions locked + what shipped

* PR #129 — revoke actually revokes (merged to main, `b047193`). Six gaps closed: `has_classroom_access` was force-kept true; `/guided/:assignmentId` and `/guided-drills/:slug` had no guard at all; `/conditional-indicators-refresher` and `/classroom/uploads` admitted any signed-in user; `has_activity_access` was missing from the DB guard trigger so students could self-grant it; permissions cached 5 min with no invalidation.
* `profiles` added to `supabase_realtime` so a flag change reaches an open tab immediately. Migration `20260922120000_profiles_realtime_and_activity_guard.sql`. Applied to the live DB via the Lovable agent.
* PR #130 — archive + permanent delete (merged to main, `dbeeac5`). Archive: all flags off, `archived_at` stamped, hidden from list and count, reversible. Delete: removes auth user, requires typed email match, primary admin protected, self-delete blocked. `student_sessions.student_id` changed from NOT Okay, so what I want you to do is, it seems like you locked me out of my own admin account. So, I want to make sure we don't lose the data on everyone's current situation and their current access. We need to ensure that we don't lose that information. Then, we can apply the necessary changes, but you can finish by asking me the questions that are needed.NULL/CASCADE to nullable/SET NULL so Joshua's session records survive a delete; `deleted_students` table + BEFORE DELETE trigger snapshots identity and backfills `former_student_id`.
* Dropped a planned `auth.admin.signOut(class_id)` call mid-build — that API takes a JWT, not a user id, so it would have silently failed on every archive. UI copy corrected to match.
* Tier design settled across 8 interview rounds. A tier is a template that sets the switches; switches remain enforcement. 12 switches, not 13. Tier 1 = all on, manual, indefinite, relabels to Tier 2 on any deviation. Tier 2 = individual toggles, persist as normal state, no snapshot restored. Tier 3 = floor (Classroom/Messages/Schedule) + assigned items only. Governing rule, stated twice by Joshua: access never changes by itself. Per item, never per feature.
* Plan written, pushed to branch `docs/student-access-tiers-plan` (`0d01fa9`), NOT merged — awaiting sign-off.

 // queryFn, which may run detached from a render.
 class SessionExpiredError extends Error {}
 
+// One realtime channel per signed-in user, shared by every mounted consumer.
+// An UPDATE to the student's own profiles row (an admin flipping a flag)
+// invalidates the cached permissions, so a revoke takes effect on the next
+// render rather than when the stale window runs out. RLS scopes the stream to
+// the student's own row; profiles is in the supabase_realtime publication
+// (migration 20260922120000).
+type ProfileWatch = { channel: ReturnType<typeof supabase.channel>; listeners: Set<() => void> };
+const profileWatches = new Map<string, ProfileWatch>();
+
+function acquireProfileWatch(userId: string, onChange: () => void): () => void {
+  let watch = profileWatches.get(userId);
+  if (!watch) {
+    const listeners = new Set<() => void>();
+    const channel = supabase
+      .channel(`profile-access-${userId}`)
+      .on(
+        "postgres_changes",
+        { event: "UPDATE", schema: "public", table: "profiles", filter: `class_id=eq.${userId}` },
+        () => listeners.forEach((fn) => fn()),
+      )
+      .subscribe();
+    watch = { channel, listeners };
+    profileWatches.set(userId, watch);
+  }
+  watch.listeners.add(onChange);
+  return onChange;
+}
+
+function releaseProfileWatch(userId: string, listener: () => void) {
+  const watch = profileWatches.get(userId);
+  if (!watch) return;
+  watch.listeners.delete(listener);
+  if (watch.listeners.size === 0) {
+    profileWatches.delete(userId);
+    void supabase.removeChannel(watch.channel);
+  }
+}
+

+  const userId = user?.id;
+
+  // The hook mounts once per ProtectedRoute, so the subscription is guarded by
+  // a single shared channel per user: the first mount opens it, the last unmount
+  // closes it. Without this, every gated page would hold its own socket.
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (!authReady || !userId) return;
+    const entry = acquireProfileWatch(userId, () => {
+      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["user-permissions", userId] });
+    });
+    return () => releaseProfileWatch(userId, entry);
+  }, [authReady, userId, queryClient]);

-    queryKey: ["user-permissions", user?.id],

+    // stale window and the focus refetch are the fallback when the socket is
+    // down, so "Revoke All Access" is never more than a tab switch away from
+    // taking effect. Cached data keeps `loading` false, so neither flashes.
+    staleTime: 60_000,

+    refetchOnWindowFocus: true,

-- Two ways to remove a student from the People page.
--
-- Archive is the everyday one: access off, hidden from the list, every record
-- kept, reversible. It is a profiles column, not a deletion.
--
-- Hard delete removes the auth user, which cascades to roughly sixty tables.
-- Two things must outlive it: Joshua's own session records, and the payment
-- and booking history. The booking tables already snapshot email and name, so
-- they read fine once detached. student_sessions did not: student_id was NOT
-- NULL and cascaded, so deleting a student destroyed the teaching record of
-- sessions he taught. That is his work product, not theirs.

-- ── Archive ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
ALTER TABLE public.profiles
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS archived_at timestamptz,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS archived_reason text;

COMMENT ON COLUMN public.profiles.archived_at IS
  'Set when a student is archived: access revoked, hidden from the People list, records kept. NULL means active.';

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS profiles_archived_at_idx
  ON public.profiles (archived_at) WHERE archived_at IS NOT NULL;

-- Archiving is an admin act, like the access flags, so students cannot clear
-- it on their own row through the "Students can update own profile" policy.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_profile_admin_fields()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF auth.uid() IS NULL OR public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin') THEN
    RETURN NEW;
  END IF;

  IF NEW.has_bootcamp_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_bootcamp_access  OR
     NEW.has_classroom_access IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_classroom_access OR
     NEW.has_analytics_access IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_analytics_access OR
     NEW.has_schedule_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_schedule_access  OR
     NEW.has_practice_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_practice_access  OR
     NEW.has_drill_access     IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_drill_access     OR
     NEW.has_waj_access       IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_waj_access       OR
     NEW.has_journal_access   IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_journal_access   OR
     NEW.has_flagged_access   IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_flagged_access   OR
     NEW.has_chat_access      IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_chat_access      OR
     NEW.has_export_access    IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_export_access    OR
     NEW.has_ta_access        IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_ta_access        OR
     NEW.has_activity_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_activity_access  OR
     NEW.archived_at          IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.archived_at          OR
     NEW.archived_reason      IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.archived_reason      OR
     NEW.proficiency_level    IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.proficiency_level    OR
     NEW.class_id             IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.class_id             THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'profiles: access flags, archive state, proficiency_level and class_id are admin-controlled';
  END IF;

  RETURN NEW;
END;
$function$;

-- ── Who was deleted ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- The surviving rows keep the old uuid in former_student_id, which is a plain
-- column and not a foreign key. This table is what makes that uuid readable
-- again, so a detached session or payment still has a name on it.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.deleted_students (
  user_id      uuid PRIMARY KEY,
  email        text,
  display_name text,
  deleted_at   timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  deleted_by   uuid,
  note         text
);

ALTER TABLE public.deleted_students ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Admins can view deleted students" ON public.deleted_students;
CREATE POLICY "Admins can view deleted students"
  ON public.deleted_students FOR SELECT
  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'));

-- ── Keep the session record ──────────────────────────────────────────────
ALTER TABLE public.student_sessions
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS former_student_id uuid;

ALTER TABLE public.student_sessions ALTER COLUMN student_id DROP NOT NULL;

DO $$
DECLARE fk_name text;
BEGIN
  SELECT c.conname INTO fk_name
  FROM pg_constraint c
  JOIN unnest(c.conkey) k(attnum) ON true
  JOIN pg_attribute a ON a.attrelid = c.conrelid AND a.attnum = k.attnum
  WHERE c.contype = 'f'
    AND c.conrelid = 'public.student_sessions'::regclass
    AND c.confrelid = 'auth.users'::regclass
    AND a.attname = 'student_id'
  LIMIT 1;

  IF fk_name IS NOT NULL THEN
    EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE public.student_sessions DROP CONSTRAINT %I', fk_name);
  END IF;

  ALTER TABLE public.student_sessions
    ADD CONSTRAINT student_sessions_student_id_fkey
    FOREIGN KEY (student_id) REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;
END $$;

-- The booking and payment tables are already ON DELETE SET NULL and already
-- carry their own email/name snapshots. They only need the old id kept so the
-- rows can still be grouped per person after the account is gone.
ALTER TABLE public.booking_passes          ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS former_student_id uuid;
ALTER TABLE public.booking_commerce_events ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS former_student_id uuid;
ALTER TABLE public.single_session_bookings ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS former_student_id uuid;
ALTER TABLE public.external_booking_events ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS former_student_id uuid;

-- ── Snapshot on the way out ──────────────────────────────────────────────
-- Runs BEFORE the delete, so it can still read the profile and still see the
-- rows that are about to be detached by the SET NULL cascades.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.snapshot_deleted_student()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO public.deleted_students (user_id, email, display_name, deleted_at)
  VALUES (
    OLD.id,
    OLD.email,
    (SELECT display_name FROM public.profiles WHERE class_id = OLD.id::text),
    now()
  )
  ON CONFLICT (user_id) DO UPDATE
    SET email = EXCLUDED.email,
        display_name = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.display_name, public.deleted_students.display_name),
        deleted_at = EXCLUDED.deleted_at;

  UPDATE public.student_sessions          SET former_student_id = OLD.id WHERE student_id = OLD.id;
  UPDATE public.booking_passes            SET former_student_id = OLD.id WHERE student_id = OLD.id;
  UPDATE public.booking_commerce_events   SET former_student_id = OLD.id WHERE student_id = OLD.id;
  UPDATE public.single_session_bookings   SET former_student_id = OLD.id WHERE user_id    = OLD.id;
  UPDATE public.external_booking_events   SET former_student_id = OLD.id WHERE student_id = OLD.id;

  RETURN OLD;
END;
$function$;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS snapshot_deleted_student_trg ON auth.users;
CREATE TRIGGER snapshot_deleted_student_trg
  BEFORE DELETE ON auth.users
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.snapshot_deleted_student();

## assistant

Your data is safe, and your admin account is intact in the database. The problem looks like a code-versus-database mismatch, not anything I changed on your account.

**What I checked on the live database (read-only, nothing changed):**
- **Your admin account is fine.** `contact@aspiringattorneys.com` still has the `admin` role, isn't banned, has every access flag on, and last signed in at 00:49 UTC. Your second admin account, `lsatmaster` / hotmail, is also still admin.
- **Every student's access is exactly as you left it.** It matches the counts from this morning: 19 full access, 6 partial, 14 classroom-only.

**The snapshot is saved.** I saved everyone's access as it stands right now to [2026-09-22-profiles-access.sql](../../raw/sources/lsatu-access-snapshots/2026-09-22-profiles-access.sql): one line per person with all 13 switches, plus both admin roles. If anything ever goes wrong, running that file puts every student back exactly as they are tonight. It changes nothing else.

**The likely cause (a strong lead, not yet proven):** PR #130's code expects two new database fields, `archived_at` and `archived_reason`. I checked, and they don't exist in the live database because that migration was never applied. The admin People page loads its list with a query that asks for `archived_at`. With the field missing, that query errors and the admin side comes up broken or empty. From your side, that looks like being locked out. I couldn't reproduce it myself because I can't sign in as you.

**The fix doesn't touch anyone's access.** I read the migration line by line. It adds the archive fields, the `deleted_students` table and the session-record safeguards. It doesn't change a single access switch for any student. The snapshot covers us regardless.

Questions before I change anything:

1. **What exactly do you see?** A blank or error People page, a "locked" screen, or being sent back to sign-in? And is this on lsatprep.study or the Lovable preview? If it's the People page or the admin desk, that confirms the cause.
2. **Should I apply the #130 migration to the live database now?** This is the fix. It adds fields only, and nobody's access changes. It goes through Lovable, which may use credits, so I need your yes first.
3. **Should I publish the frontend to lsatprep.study afterward?** Right now I can't confirm what production is running.
4. **The 14 classroom-only students** (Nicole, Fernande, Cameron, Avneet, Whitney, Andrew, Antonio, Favour, Lexi A, Reet, C. Alexander, Isabella, Lydia, Kaden): leave them exactly as they are for now, as you asked? The tiers plan would later move them to Tier 3, which gives them Messages and Schedule back.
5. **Tiers plan:** approve it and choose which stage comes first, or hold it until the lockout is fixed? I'd hold it.

## assistant

Nobody's access changed. The database compared all 41 accounts against the snapshot I saved before we touched anything, and all 41 match exactly on all 13 access switches.

- **Everyone who had access still has it,** switch for switch: the 19 with full access, and the 6 with partial access (Sandra, Ivory Johnson, Venusa, Zeina, Meghan Mullins, Priyanka) with the same exact mix of switches as before.
- **Everyone who didn't have access still doesn't.** The 14 classroom-only students (Nicole, Fernande, Cameron, Avneet, Whitney, Andrew, Antonio, Favour, Lexi A, Reet, C. Alexander, Isabella, Lydia, Kaden) are unchanged.
- **No accounts were added or removed.** There were 41 before and 41 now.
- **Your two admin accounts are both still admin:** `contact@aspiringattorneys.com` and `lsatmaster` / hotmail.

I checked access switches and admin roles only. Assignments and student work weren't part of this check, but nothing we did touches them.

One thing I can't explain yet: the database hasn't changed. The archive migration still isn't applied, so whatever fixed your lockout happened on the app side, not in the data. If the People page acts up again, that migration is still the likely cause. I haven't applied it, and I won't unless you say so.

The snapshot stays at `raw/sources/lsatu-access-snapshots/2026-09-22-profiles-access.sql`. If anything ever changes by accident, running it puts all 41 accounts back to tonight's switches.

## assistant

Five things are left. The first three each need a yes from you before I run them. The last two are decisions only.

**1. Apply the archive/delete database change (PR #130).**
The Archive and Delete buttons are already in the code, but the database is missing the fields they need. Until this runs, those buttons fail and the People page is fragile. This is probably what locked you out earlier.
- It only adds fields and a safety table. It changes nobody's access, and I'll re-run the 41-account comparison afterward to prove it.
- It goes through the Lovable agent, which may spend credits.

**2. Deploy the updated `admin-manage-users` function.**
This is the server code behind Revoke, Archive and Delete. Pushing code doesn't deploy a function, and I have never confirmed the live version is the new one. It also goes through Lovable, so it may spend credits. I'd send it in the same Lovable message as #1, so it costs one round.

**3. Publish the frontend to lsatprep.study.**
Nothing from PR #129 or #130 is confirmed live. Until this runs, "Revoke All Access" on the real site may still be the old, leaky version. Publishing is a separate free step, and I'd run it right after #1 and #2.

**4. The 14 classroom-only students.**
I'd leave them exactly as they are, per your instruction tonight, and handle them when tiers ship.

**5. The access tiers plan.**

docs/student-access-tiers-plan
# Student Access Tiers — implementation plan

Date: 2026-09-22
Status: DRAFT, awaiting Joshua's sign-off
Owner: Jay 2
Source: `~/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-22-student-access-tiers.md` (full interview record)
Scope: the 13 `has_*_access` flags, a new tier layer above them, per-item assignment authorisation, the signup queue, and removal of the payment auto-grant.

---

## 1. What Joshua asked for

1. Three tiers he applies himself, like templates.
2. **Tier 1** — every feature on, indefinite.
3. **Tier 2** — he picks each feature, toggled individually, effect is instant.
4. **Tier 3** — strictly what he assigns, and nothing else.
5. A signup queue on his admin homepage with a checkmark / X per person.
6. Switch labels in plain English that say what they actually open.

## 2. Decisions locked in the grill (2026-09-22)

| # | Decision |
|---|---|
| 1 | A tier is a **template**. It sets the switches; the switches stay what the app checks. |
| 2 | **One tier per student.** No stacking. |
| 3 | **12 switches**, not 13 — the two Wrong answer switches merge. |
| 4 | Tier 1 = all 12 on, manual only, never expires. |
| 5 | Tier 1 is all-on **by definition**; turning any switch off relabels the student Tier 2. Label only, never access. |
| 6 | Tier 2 toggles persist as normal state. No snapshot is restored on leaving and returning. |
| 7 | Tier 3 = the floor plus assigned items only. |
| 8 | **The floor** = Classroom home, Messages, Schedule. |
| 9 | **Per item, never per feature.** Assigning one practice test must not open the Testing Centre. |
| 10 | Assigned work **authorises its own runner**, following the existing `BootcampGate` pattern. |
| 11 | **Access never changes by itself.** No expiry, no automatic upgrade or downgrade. |
| 12 | Payment no longer grants access. It raises a notification instead. |
| 13 | Signup queue lives on **both** Today and Students. |
| 14 | Queue holds new signups **and** everyone with no tier. |
| 15 | Checkmark assigns a tier. **X puts them on Tier 3**, which is functionally no access. |
| 16 | "No tier" = undecided. Same access as Tier 3, but sitting in the queue. |
| 17 | Migration: 19 → Tier 1, 6 → Tier 2 with toggles kept, 14 → Tier 3 **and they regain Messages and Schedule**. |
| 18 | A "practice test" means the **full sitting**. A section is a separate assignable kind. |
| 19 | Students never see tier names. |
| 20 | No history of tier changes. |
| 21 | Tiers control feature access only — nothing to do with pricing, rates or session length. |

## 3. What exists today (verified 2026-09-22)

- 13 boolean columns on `profiles`, **all defaulting to `true`**. A new signup gets the whole platform.
- `useUserPermissions` reads them; `ProtectedRoute` gates routes; `railCatalog` padlocks rail items. Admins bypass everything.
- `profiles` is in the `supabase_realtime` publication, so a switch change reaches an open tab immediately. **Joshua's "instant toggle" requirement is already satisfied.**
- `BootcampGate` is a working per-item gate: `/bootcamp/*` routes carry **no `ProtectedRoute` and no flag**, only an assignment check. This is the proven pattern Tier 3 copies.
- `grantStudentAccess.ts` fires on Stripe `checkout.session.completed` and switches on 9 of 13 flags automatically. This contradicts decision 11 and is removed in Stage 4.
- Per-feature toggle UI and Trial/Standard/Full templates already exist in `adminUsers.ts` and `AdminDashboard.tsx`, but `AdminDashboard` is **not mounted on any route**.
- `practice_test_runs` is self-started (`user_id` defaults to `auth.uid()`). There is **no** assignment path for a full practice test.

### The four mislabelled switches

| Label today | What it really gates | Target |
|---|---|---|
| Practice tests (`has_practice_access`) | `/sets` (saved Question Sets) | The Testing Centre |
| TA assignments (`has_ta_access`) | nothing — `/classroom/ta` uses `has_classroom_access` | TA worksheets |
| Wrong answers (`has_journal_access`) | `/journal`, opens if **either** journal flag is on | `/journal`, alone |
| Wrong answer journal (`has_waj_access`) | same page | retired |

## 4. Target switch set (12)

| Group | Label | Column | Gates |
|---|---|---|---|
| Practice | Drill | `has_drill_access` | `/drill`, `/rc*`, `/sets`, guided drill runners |
| Practice | Practice tests | `has_practice_access` | `/testing`, `/testing/test/:runId` |
| Practice | Activities | `has_activity_access` | `/activities*` |
| Practice | Bootcamps | `has_bootcamp_access` | `/bootcamps` index only |
| Review | Wrong answers | `has_journal_access` | `/journal*` |
| Review | Flagged questions | `has_flagged_access` | `/flagged*` |
| Review | Progress and history | `has_analytics_access` | `/analytics`, `/history` |
| Review | Export | `has_export_access` | export controls |
| Classroom | Classroom | `has_classroom_access` | classroom contents |
| Classroom | Messages | `has_chat_access` | `/inbox*` |
| Classroom | Schedule and booking | `has_schedule_access` | `/schedule`, `/booking` |
| Classroom | TA worksheets | `has_ta_access` | `/classroom/ta/:assignmentId` |

---

## 5. Build order

Stages 1–5 deliver a working tier system. Stage 6 is what makes Tier 3 mean what Joshua said. Stage 7 is the only genuinely new feature. Each stage is independently shippable and independently revertible.

### Stage 1 — Rewire the switches, merge the journal pair

**Code**
- `/testing` and `/testing/test/:runId`: `has_drill_access` → `has_practice_access`.
- `/sets`: `has_practice_access` → `has_drill_access`.
- `/classroom/ta/:assignmentId`: `has_classroom_access` → `has_ta_access`.
- `/journal`, `/journal/:qid`: drop the `has_waj_access` alternative, read `has_journal_access` only.
- `adminUsers.ts`: relabel per section 4, drop `has_waj_access` from the groups.
- `railCatalog.ts`: update the Testing Centre entry to `has_practice_access`.

**Migration — preserve effective access exactly**
```sql
UPDATE profiles SET
  has_practice_access = has_drill_access,              -- Testing Centre was drill-gated
  has_ta_access       = has_classroom_access,          -- TA route was classroom-gated
  has_journal_access  = has_journal_access OR has_waj_access;
```
`has_waj_access` is left in place but no longer read. Drop it in a later cleanup once the change has held.

**Known exception, and why it is safe.** Two students (Meghan Mullins, priyanka_nambiar@brown.edu) have Practice on and Drill off. Moving `/sets` under Drill means they lose saved Question Sets. A saved set without Drill is already a dead end — opening one launches `/drill`, which they cannot reach — so nothing usable is lost. No other student is affected.

**Verify.** Write a one-off script that computes every student's reachable routes before and after, and diff. The only permitted difference is the `/sets` case above.

### Stage 2 — The tier column and the template engine

**Migration**
- `profiles.access_tier text` — `NULL | 'tier_1' | 'tier_2' | 'tier_3'`, with a CHECK constraint. NULL means undecided.
- Add `access_tier` to `guard_profile_admin_fields()` so students cannot set it on their own row.
- **Drift trigger** (BEFORE UPDATE on `profiles`): if the row is `tier_1` and any of the 12 switches moves to false, set `access_tier = 'tier_2'`. Implemented in the database so it holds no matter which code path writes.

**Edge function** — `admin-manage-users` gains `set_tier`:
- `tier_1` → all 12 on.
- `tier_2` → leave switches untouched; record the label only.
- `tier_3` → floor on (`classroom`, `chat`, `schedule`), the other nine off.

**UI** — tier control on the Students page, in the expanded panel next to the existing access section. Selecting Tier 2 reveals the 12 toggles grouped as in section 4. Existing single-flag `toggle` action drives them; realtime already makes the effect instant.

### Stage 3 — Migrate the 41, change the defaults

**Migration**
```sql
-- Tier by current state, computed AFTER Stage 1's rewire
UPDATE profiles SET access_tier = 'tier_1' WHERE <all 12 on>;
UPDATE profiles SET access_tier = 'tier_3' WHERE <classroom only>;
UPDATE profiles SET access_tier = 'tier_2' WHERE access_tier IS NULL AND <not archived>;

-- The one deliberate widening (decision 17)
UPDATE profiles SET has_chat_access = true, has_schedule_access = true
WHERE access_tier = 'tier_3';

-- New signups start at the floor, undecided
ALTER TABLE profiles
  ALTER COLUMN has_classroom_access SET DEFAULT true,
  ALTER COLUMN has_chat_access      SET DEFAULT true,
  ALTER COLUMN has_schedule_access  SET DEFAULT true,
  ALTER COLUMN has_drill_access     SET DEFAULT false,
  -- ...and false for the remaining six
  ALTER COLUMN access_tier          SET DEFAULT NULL;
```

This is the change that finally closes the open front door: a stranger who signs up gets the floor and nothing else.

### Stage 4 — Payment stops granting access

- Remove the `grantStudentAccess` call from `booking-commerce-webhook/index.ts`.
- In its place, insert a `notifications` row (`type = 'payment_needs_tier'`) carrying the payer's email and amount.
- Delete `_shared/grantStudentAccess.ts` once nothing imports it.

**Risk worth stating plainly.** Until Stage 5 ships, a student who pays gets no access until Joshua acts. Stages 4 and 5 should ship together.

### Stage 5 — The signup queue

- Query: `profiles WHERE access_tier IS NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`, newest first, plus unread `type='signup'` notifications.
- One shared component rendered on **Today** and on **Students**.
- Per row: name, email, signup date, and two controls — a checkmark opening a Tier 1 / 2 / 3 picker, and an X that applies `tier_3`.
- Both write through `set_tier`, so the queue empties as decisions are made.

### Stage 6 — Per-item authorisation (this is what makes Tier 3 real)

A shared `<AssignmentGate>` modelled on `BootcampGate`: it checks that the signed-in student owns an assignment row of the right kind for the id in the route, renders the child if so, and otherwise falls through to the ordinary flag check. Admins bypass, as `BootcampGate` already does.

Applied to:
- `/section/:assignmentId` and `/classroom/:assignmentId` — homework and timed sections.
- `/classroom/item/:itemId` — the generic feed item.
- `/classroom/ta/:assignmentId` — TA worksheets.
- `/activities/:assignmentId` — Activities.
- `/guided/:assignmentId`, `/guided-drills/:slug` — guided drills.

**The hard part:** `/drill` is a shared runner behind `DrillLaunchGate`. It must accept a run that is authorised by an assignment rather than by `has_drill_access`. `launchDrill({kind:'assignment'})` already carries the assignment identity, so the gate reads that and authorises the single run — without opening `/drill` generally.

**Note on security.** These gates are route-level. The real protection on the data is RLS, which already scopes every assignment table by `student_id`. A Tier 3 student cannot read someone else's work regardless of this stage.

### Stage 7 — Assignable practice tests (new build)

The only part with no existing foundation.

- New table `practice_test_assignments`: `student_id`, `pt`, `assigned_by`, `assigned_at`, `unlock_at`, `status`, with RLS matching the other per-student assignment tables.
- A matching `classroom_items` row, `type = 'practice_test'`, so it appears in Assigned work like everything else.
- `TestingCentre` lists **only assigned PrepTests** for a student without `has_practice_access`, and the whole bank for one with it.
- `/testing/test/:runId` wrapped in `AssignmentGate`.
- The `practice_test_runs` INSERT policy must require either `has_practice_access` or a matching assignment row, so the API cannot be used to start an unassigned test.

---

## 6. Risks

| Risk | Mitigation |
|---|---|
| Stage 1 silently changes who can reach what | Before/after reachability diff per student; only the documented `/sets` case may differ |
| Stage 3 changes column defaults — a mistake locks out every new signup | Ship behind Stage 5 so the queue exists to fix it, and test a real signup end to end |
| Stage 4 strands a paying student | Ship Stages 4 and 5 together, never 4 alone |
| Drift trigger fires on admin bulk updates and mass-demotes | Trigger checks for a transition to false on a `tier_1` row only; covered by a test |
| Tier 3 student hits a dead runner | Stage 6 covers all six routes at once; a partial Stage 6 reintroduces the exact bug this work exists to fix |

## 7. Open items, flagged not assumed

1. **Tier 3 drift.** Decision 5 covers Tier 1 only. If a Tier 3 student has a switch turned on beyond the floor, do they become Tier 2? Consistency says yes. **Not confirmed — needs Joshua.**
2. **Dropping `has_waj_access`.** Kept for now. Needs a follow-up cleanup once Stage 1 has held.
3. **Stage 7 sequencing.** Joshua declined to defer it. It is still the largest piece; if tiers are wanted sooner, this is the natural split point.

## 8. Out of scope

Student-visible tier names, tier change history, and any link between tiers and pricing, booking rates or session length — all explicitly declined (decisions 19, 20, 21).

## 9. Prerequisites outside this plan

1. The PR #130 migration is not applied to the live database.
2. The frontend is not published to lsatprep.study.

Both block anything here from being visible.

const LS_KEY = "ta-selected-student";

const HIDDEN_ROUTES = ["/classroom/drill", "/drill"];

export default function FloatingTAWidget() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  const { user, authReady } = useAuth();
  const { is_admin, has_ta_access, loading: permsLoading } = useUserPermissions();

  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => subscribeTAWidget(() => setOpen(true)), []);

  const fabHidden = HIDDEN_ROUTES.some(
    (r) => location.pathname === r || location.pathname.startsWith(`${r}/`),
  );
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string | null>(() => {
    try {
      return localStorage.getItem(LS_KEY);
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      if (selected) localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY, selected);
    } catch {
      /* ignore */
    }
  }, [selected]);

  const { data: selectedProfile } = useQuery<{ display_name: string | null } | null>({
    queryKey: ["ta-student-name", selected],
    enabled: !!selected && open,
    queryFn: async () => {
      if (!selected) return null;
      const { data } = await supabase
        .from("profiles")
        .select("display_name")
        .eq("class_id", selected)
        .maybeSingle();
      return data ?? null;
    },
  });

  const selectedName = useMemo(
    () => selectedProfile?.display_name ?? null,
    [selectedProfile]
  );

  if (!authReady || !user || permsLoading) return null;
  // Admins always get the widget; has_ta_access is for any future non-admin
  // staff role. Mirrors ProtectedRoute's `is_admin || flag` convention.
  if (!is_admin && !has_ta_access) return null;

  const openFullPage = () => {
    setOpen(false);
    navigate("/admin/ta");
  };

export const PRIMARY_ADMIN_EMAIL = 'contact@aspiringattorneys.com';

export const isPrimaryAdmin = (email: string | null | undefined) =>
  (email || '').toLowerCase() === PRIMARY_ADMIN_EMAIL;

export interface ManagedUser {
  class_id: string;
  email: string;
  display_name: string | null;
  role: string;
  last_seen_at: string | null;
  /** Set when the student has been archived: access off, hidden from the list, records kept. */
  archived_at: string | null;
  archived_reason: string | null;
  has_bootcamp_access: boolean;
  has_classroom_access: boolean;
  has_analytics_access: boolean;
  has_schedule_access: boolean;
  has_practice_access: boolean;
  has_drill_access: boolean;
  has_waj_access: boolean;
  has_flagged_access: boolean;
  has_chat_access: boolean;
  has_export_access: boolean;
  has_ta_access: boolean;
  has_journal_access: boolean;
  has_activity_access: boolean;
}

export interface AdminAnalytics {
  total_users: number;
  active_today: number;
  active_week: number;
  admin_count: number;
  feature_adoption: Record<string, number>;
  total_attempts: number;
  week_attempts: number;
}

// Every door a student can be given, grouped the way the classroom rail groups
// them, and labelled in the words the rail uses. This list is the admin UI: a
// flag that is missing here has no toggle anywhere, which is how
// `has_journal_access` and `has_activity_access` came to be ungrantable while
// the rail happily padlocked Wrong answers and Activities against them.
//
// Keep it in step with `ALL_FLAGS` in supabase/functions/admin-manage-users.
// The server writes what it is told; if the client cannot name a flag, no
// amount of pressing "grant all" will turn it on where Joshua can see it.
export const FLAG_GROUPS = [
  {
    label: 'Practice',
    flags: [
      { key: 'has_drill_access', label: 'Drill' },
      { key: 'has_practice_access', label: 'Practice tests' },
      { key: 'has_activity_access', label: 'Activities' },
      { key: 'has_bootcamp_access', label: 'Bootcamps' },
    ],
  },
  {
    label: 'Review',
    flags: [
      { key: 'has_journal_access', label: 'Wrong answers' },
      { key: 'has_waj_access', label: 'Wrong answer journal' },
      { key: 'has_flagged_access', label: 'Flagged' },
      { key: 'has_analytics_access', label: 'Analytics and History' },
      { key: 'has_export_access', label: 'Export' },
    ],
  },
  {
    label: 'Classroom',
    flags: [
      { key: 'has_classroom_access', label: 'Classroom' },
      { key: 'has_chat_access', label: 'Messages' },
      { key: 'has_schedule_access', label: 'Schedule' },
      { key: 'has_ta_access', label: 'TA assignments' },
    ],
  },
] as const;

export const CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS = FLAG_GROUPS.flatMap((group) => group.flags.map((flag) => flag.key));
/**
 * What a paying student gets regardless of which template is applied. Templates
 * force these back on after the explicit list, so "Trial" can narrow practice
 * without taking away the room the student stands in.
 */
export const DEFAULT_ON_FLAG_KEYS = [
  'has_practice_access',
  'has_bootcamp_access',
  'has_classroom_access',
  'has_analytics_access',
  'has_schedule_access',
  'has_waj_access',
  'has_journal_access',
  'has_flagged_access',
  'has_chat_access',
  'has_export_access',
  'has_ta_access',
  'has_activity_access',
] as const;
/** Deduped: every configurable flag is now also a default-on candidate. */
export const ALL_FLAG_KEYS = [...new Set<string>([...CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS, ...DEFAULT_ON_FLAG_KEYS])];

export const ALL_FLAG_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {};
FLAG_GROUPS.forEach((group) => group.flags.forEach((flag) => {
  ALL_FLAG_LABELS[flag.key] = flag.label;
}));

export function accessTemplate(enabled: string[]): Record<string, boolean> {
  const out: Record<string, boolean> = {};
  for (const key of CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS) out[key] = enabled.includes(key);
  for (const key of DEFAULT_ON_FLAG_KEYS) out[key] = true;
  return out;
}

export const ROLE_TEMPLATES: { id: string; label: string; description: string; flags: Record<string, boolean> }[] = [
  {
    id: 'trial',
    label: 'Trial',
    description: 'Practice and Drill only.',
    flags: accessTemplate(['has_practice_access', 'has_drill_access']),
  },
  {
    id: 'standard',
    label: 'Standard',
    description: 'Core tools plus classroom.',
    flags: accessTemplate([
      'has_practice_access',
      'has_drill_access',
      'has_classroom_access',
      'has_bootcamp_access',
    ]),
  },
  {
    id: 'full',
    label: 'Full',
    description: 'All configurable access.',
    flags: accessTemplate(CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS),
  },
];

export function templateMatches(user: Record<string, unknown>, template: Record<string, boolean>): boolean {
  return CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS.every((key) => Boolean(user[key]) === template[key]);
}

export function computeAdminAnalytics(users: ManagedUser[], analytics: AdminAnalytics | null): AdminAnalytics {
  return analytics || {
    total_users: users.length,
    active_today: users.filter((user) => {
      if (!user.last_seen_at) return false;
      return Date.now() - new Date(user.last_seen_at).getTime() < 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
    }).length,
    active_week: users.filter((user) => {
      if (!user.last_seen_at) return false;
      return Date.now() - new Date(user.last_seen_at).getTime() < 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
    }).length,
    admin_count: users.filter((user) => user.role === 'admin').length,
    feature_adoption: ALL_FLAG_KEYS.reduce((acc, key) => {
      acc[key] = users.filter((user) => user[key as keyof ManagedUser] === true).length;
      return acc;
    }, {} as Record<string, number>),
    total_attempts: 0,
    week_attempts: 0,
  };
}

import { createClient } from "npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2";

const corsHeaders = {
  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type",
};

// The primary admin account can never be demoted, so the system always retains
// at least one admin and can never be locked out of role management.
const PRIMARY_ADMIN_EMAIL = "contact@aspiringattorneys.com";

const ALL_FLAGS = [
  "has_bootcamp_access",
  "has_classroom_access",
  "has_analytics_access",
  "has_schedule_access",
  "has_practice_access",
  "has_drill_access",
  "has_waj_access",
  "has_flagged_access",
  "has_chat_access",
  "has_export_access",
  "has_ta_access",
  "has_journal_access",
  "has_activity_access",
];

async function verifyAdmin(req: Request) {
  const supabaseUrl = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!;
  const serviceRoleKey = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY")!;
  const anonKey = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_ANON_KEY")!;

  const authHeader = req.headers.get("Authorization");
  if (!authHeader?.startsWith("Bearer ")) throw new Error("Missing auth");
  const token = authHeader.slice("Bearer ".length);

  const anonClient = createClient(supabaseUrl, anonKey);
  const { data: claimsData, error } = await anonClient.auth.getClaims(token);
  if (error || !claimsData?.claims?.sub) throw new Error("Unauthorized");
  const user = { id: claimsData.claims.sub as string };

  const admin = createClient(supabaseUrl, serviceRoleKey);
  const { data: roleData, error: roleLookupError } = await admin
    .from("user_roles")
    .select("role")
    .eq("user_id", user.id)
    .eq("role", "admin")
    .maybeSingle();

  if (roleLookupError) {
    console.error("verifyAdmin: role lookup failed", {
      user_id: user.id,
      error: roleLookupError,
    });
    throw new Error("Forbidden");
  }
  if (!roleData) throw new Error("Forbidden");

  return { admin, callerId: user.id };

        ...p,
        email: emailMap.get(p.class_id) || "unknown",

      }));

      return jsonRes(result);
    }

    // ── POST: toggle flag, bulk actions, role management ──
    if (req.method === "POST") {
      const body = await req.json();
      const { action } = body;

      // Toggle single flag
      if (!action || action === "toggle") {
        const { class_id, field, value } = body;
        if (!class_id || !ALL_FLAGS.includes(field) || typeof value !== "boolean") {
          return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);
        }
        // Return the row we actually wrote. A filter that matches nothing is
        // not an error in PostgREST, so without this the dashboard would show
        // a successful toggle that never landed and then snap back on reload.
        const { data: updated, error } = await admin
          .from("profiles")
          .update({ [field]: value })
          .eq("class_id", class_id)
          .select("class_id, " + field);
        if (error) throw error;
        if (!updated || updated.length === 0) {
          console.error("toggle: no profile matched", { class_id, field, value });
          return jsonRes({ error: "No profile matched that class_id" }, 404);
        }
        return jsonRes({ success: true, profile: updated[0] });
      }

      // Apply a template (explicit flag map) to a user. One DB update so
      // the user never sits in a partially-applied state.
      if (action === "apply_template") {
        const { class_id, flags } = body;
        if (!class_id || !flags || typeof flags !== "object") {
          return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);
        }
        const updates: Record<string, boolean> = {};
        for (const flag of ALL_FLAGS) {
          if (typeof flags[flag] === "boolean") {
            updates[flag] = flags[flag];
          }
        }
        if (Object.keys(updates).length === 0) {
          return jsonRes({ error: "No valid flags" }, 400);
        }
        const { error } = await admin
          .from("profiles")
          .update(updates)
          .eq("class_id", class_id);
        if (error) throw error;
        return jsonRes({ success: true });
      }

      // Bulk grant/revoke all flags for a user
      if (action === "bulk_user") {
        const { class_id, value } = body;
        if (!class_id || typeof value !== "boolean") {
          return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);
        }
        const updates: Record<string, boolean> = {};
        for (const flag of ALL_FLAGS) updates[flag] = value;
        const { error } = await admin
          .from("profiles")
          .update(updates)
          .eq("class_id", class_id);
        if (error) throw error;
        return jsonRes({ success: true });
      }

      // Put an account back to the state a brand-new signup is in: no intake
      // answers, no name, the intake email re-armed, and every paid door shut.
      // Built for testing the signup path end to end without hand-written SQL,
      // and for the case where a student needs to answer the intake again.
      //
      // Deliberately does not touch auth: the account, its password and its
      // work all survive. Nothing here deletes a student's practice history.
      if (action === "reset_onboarding") {
        const { class_id, lock = true } = body;
        if (!class_id) return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);

        const updates: Record<string, unknown> = {
          onboarding: null,
          onboarding_completed_at: null,
          onboarding_notified_at: null,
          display_name: null,
            throw deleteError;
          }
        }
        return jsonRes({ success: true });
      }

      // Archive: the everyday way to remove someone from the People page.
      // Every door shut, stamped archived, nothing destroyed. Reversible.
      if (action === "archive_student") {
        const { class_id, reason = null } = body;
        if (!class_id) return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);

        const { data: target } = await admin.auth.admin.getUserById(class_id);
        if ((target?.user?.email || "").toLowerCase() === PRIMARY_ADMIN_EMAIL) {
          return jsonRes({ error: "The primary admin account cannot be archived." }, 403);
        }

        const updates: Record<string, unknown> = {
          archived_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          archived_reason: reason,
        };
        for (const flag of ALL_FLAGS) updates[flag] = false;

        const { data: archived, error } = await admin
          .from("profiles")
          .update(updates)
          .eq("class_id", class_id)
          .select("class_id, display_name, archived_at");
        if (error) throw error;
        if (!archived || archived.length === 0) {
          console.error("archive_student: no profile matched", { class_id });
          return jsonRes({ error: "No profile matched that class_id" }, 404);
        }

        // An open tab does not need to be signed out for this to bite: the
        // profiles row is in the realtime publication, so useUserPermissions
        // invalidates on this UPDATE and every door closes on the next render.
        // (auth.admin.signOut takes a JWT, not a user id, so there is no
        // server-side way to end their session from here.)
        return jsonRes({ success: true, profile: archived[0] });
      }

      // Unarchive puts them back in the list. Access stays off on purpose:
      // coming back is a decision about which doors to reopen, and silently
      // restoring all thirteen would be a bigger act than the button implies.
      if (action === "unarchive_student") {
        const { class_id } = body;
        if (!class_id) return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);

        const { data: restored, error } = await admin
          .from("profiles")

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
  Activity,
  AlertTriangle,
  BarChart3,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronUp,
  Crown,
  RotateCcw,
  Search,
  Shield,
  User,
  UserCheck,
  Users,
  UserX,
  Archive,
  ArchiveRestore,
  Trash2,
  CalendarDays,
  Video,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Skeleton } from '@/components/ui/skeleton';
import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
import { useHubStudents, type HubStudentRow } from '@/hooks/useHubStudents';
import {
  ALL_FLAG_KEYS,
  CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS,
  computeAdminAnalytics,
  isPrimaryAdmin,
  type AdminAnalytics,
  type ManagedUser,
} from '@/lib/adminUsers';
import { formatLastActiveLabel, isUserOnline } from '@/lib/presence';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';

type RoleFilter = 'all' | 'students' | 'admins' | 'no-drill' | 'archived';

type PeopleRow = ManagedUser & {
  student?: HubStudentRow;
};

type GlobalSingleSession = {
  source: 'single_session';
  id: string;
  start_at: string;
  end_at: string;
  status: string;
  meet_url: string | null;
  name: string | null;
  email: string | null;
  user_id: string | null;
};

        (roleFilter === 'admins' && person.role === 'admin') ||
        (roleFilter === 'no-drill' && person.role !== 'admin' && !person.has_drill_access);
      return matchesSearch && matchesRole;
    });
  }, [people, roleFilter, search]);

  const toggleAccess = async (classId: string, field: string, value: boolean) => {
    setUsers((prev) => prev.map((user) => (user.class_id === classId ? { ...user, [field]: value } : user)));
    try {
      const res = await supabase.functions.invoke('admin-manage-users', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: { class_id: classId, field, value },
      });
      if (res.error) throw res.error;
      // Trust the row the server wrote back rather than the optimistic guess,
      // so a toggle that never landed can't look like it worked.
      const written = (res.data as { profile?: Record<string, unknown> } | null)?.profile;
      if (!written || written[field] !== value) throw new Error('Permission was not saved');
    } catch (error) {
      setUsers((prev) => prev.map((user) => (user.class_id === classId ? { ...user, [field]: !value } : user)));
      console.error('toggleAccess failed', { classId, field, value, error });
      toast.error('Failed to update permission');
    }
  };

  // One button, whole account. Revoking turns every flag off, classroom
  // included: the /classroom route lets any signed-in user land, so the
  // student still has somewhere to stand, but the home page shows them an
  // "access paused" notice instead of their coursework. Leaving classroom on
  // used to keep assigned work, notes and recaps open after a revoke.
  // Granting opens everything back up.
  const setAllAccess = async (person: PeopleRow, grant: boolean) => {
    const who = person.display_name || person.email;
    if (!grant && !window.confirm(
      `Revoke all access for ${who}?

` +
      'They keep their login. Everything else, including assigned work, ' +
      'notes, drill, bootcamps, review and messages, is locked until you grant it back.',
    )) return;

    const flags: Record<string, boolean> = {};
    for (const key of ALL_FLAG_KEYS) flags[key] = grant;

    const previous = users;
    setUsers((prev) => prev.map((user) => (user.class_id === person.class_id ? { ...user, ...flags } : user)));
    try {
      const res = await supabase.functions.invoke('admin-manage-users', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: { action: 'apply_template', class_id: person.class_id, flags },
      });
      if (res.error) throw res.error;
      toast.success(grant ? `${who} has full access` : `${who} has no access`);
      await fetchData();
    } catch (error) {
      setUsers(previous);
      console.error('setAllAccess failed', { class_id: person.class_id, grant, error });
      toast.error('Failed to update access');
    }
  };

  // Puts an account back to the state a new signup is in: no intake answers, no
  // name, the intake email re-armed, every paid door shut. Used to test the
  // signup path for real, and when a student needs to answer the intake again.
  const resetOnboarding = async (person: PeopleRow) => {
    const who = person.display_name || person.email;
    if (!window.confirm(
      `Reset ${who} to a brand-new account?\n\n` +
      'Clears their setup answers and name, re-locks access, and lets the ' +
      'intake run again. Their login and their practice history are untouched.',
    )) return;

    try {
      const res = await supabase.functions.invoke('admin-manage-users', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: { action: 'reset_onboarding', class_id: person.class_id },
      });
      if (res.error) throw res.error;
      toast.success(`${who} is back to a new account`);
      await fetchData();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('resetOnboarding failed', { class_id: person.class_id, error });
      toast.error('Failed to reset the account');
    }
  };

  // Archive: the everyday way to take someone off the list. Shuts every door,
  // takes effect on their open tab immediately, keeps every record, undoable.
  const setArchived = async (person: PeopleRow, archived: boolean) => {
    const who = person.display_name || person.email;
    if (archived && !window.confirm(
      `Archive ${who}?\n\n` +
      'Their access is turned off and they come off your list. Nothing is ' +
      'deleted: their work, sessions and history all stay, and you can bring ' +
      'them back at any time.',
    )) return;
          expandedUser={expandedUser}
          setExpandedUser={setExpandedUser}
          toggleAccess={toggleAccess}
          setAllAccess={setAllAccess}
          setRole={setRole}
          resetOnboarding={resetOnboarding}
          setArchived={setArchived}
          deleteStudent={deleteStudent}
          refresh={fetchData}
        />
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function PeopleList({
  filtered,
  studentsLoading,
  expandedUser,
  setExpandedUser,
  toggleAccess,
  setAllAccess,
  setRole,
  resetOnboarding,
  setArchived,
  deleteStudent,
  refresh,
}: {
  filtered: PeopleRow[];
  studentsLoading: boolean;
  expandedUser: string | null;
  setExpandedUser: (id: string | null) => void;
  toggleAccess: (classId: string, field: string, value: boolean) => void;
  setAllAccess: (person: PeopleRow, grant: boolean) => void;
  setRole: (userId: string, role: 'admin' | 'user') => void;
  resetOnboarding: (person: PeopleRow) => void;
  setArchived: (person: PeopleRow, archived: boolean) => void;
  deleteStudent: (person: PeopleRow) => void;
  refresh: () => void;
}) {
  if (studentsLoading && filtered.length === 0) {
    return (
      <ul className="mt-4 divide-y divide-black/10 border border-black/10" aria-label="Loading people">
        {[0, 1, 2, 3].map((item) => (
          <li key={item} className="flex items-center gap-3 px-5 py-4">
            <Skeleton className="h-9 w-9 rounded-full bg-black/10" />
            <div className="flex-1 space-y-2">
              <Skeleton className="h-3 w-1/2 bg-black/10" />
              <Skeleton className="h-3 w-1/3 bg-black/10" />
            </div>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    );
  }

  if (filtered.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="mt-4 border border-black/10 px-5 py-8 text-center text-sm text-black/45">
        <div className="flex justify-center">
          <AlertTriangle className="h-4 w-4" strokeWidth={1.5} />
        </div>
        <p className="mt-2">No people found.</p>
        <button type="button" onClick={refresh} className="mt-3 text-xs font-semibold text-black/55 underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-black">
          Refresh
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="mt-4 overflow-hidden border border-black/10">
      <div className="divide-y divide-black/10">
        {filtered.map((person) => {
          const isExpanded = expandedUser === person.class_id;
          return (
            <div key={person.class_id} data-testid={`people-row-${person.class_id}`} className="bg-white/10">
              <PersonRow person={person} isExpanded={isExpanded} setExpandedUser={setExpandedUser} setAllAccess={setAllAccess} />
              {isExpanded && (
                <PersonAccessPanel
                  person={person}
                  setRole={setRole}
                  resetOnboarding={resetOnboarding}
                  setArchived={setArchived}
                  deleteStudent={deleteStudent}
                />
              )}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function PersonRow({
  person,
  isExpanded,
  setExpandedUser,
  setAllAccess,
}: {
  person: PeopleRow;
  isExpanded: boolean;
  setExpandedUser: (id: string | null) => void;
  setAllAccess: (person: PeopleRow, grant: boolean) => void;
}) {
  const student = person.student;
  const hasDrill = Boolean(person.has_drill_access);
  // "Any access" ignores the classroom home page, which revoking always leaves on.
  const hasAnyAccess = ALL_FLAG_KEYS.some(
    (key) => key !== 'has_classroom_access' && Boolean((person as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)[key]),
  );
  const isAdmin = person.role === 'admin';
  const isOnline = isUserOnline(person.last_seen_at);
  const lastActiveLabel = formatLastActiveLabel(person.last_seen_at);
  const displayName = person.display_name || person.email;

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-[auto_minmax(0,1fr)_auto] items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3">
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setExpandedUser(isExpanded ? null : person.class_id)}
        className="text-black/45 transition hover:text-black"
        aria-label={isExpanded ? `Collapse ${displayName}` : `Expand ${displayName}`}
      >
        {isExpanded ? <ChevronUp className="h-4 w-4" strokeWidth={1.5} /> : <ChevronDown className="h-4 w-4" strokeWidth={1.5} />}
      </button>
      <div className="flex min-w-0 items-start gap-3">
        <div className="relative flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-black/15 bg-white/25">
          <User className="h-4 w-4 text-black/45" strokeWidth={1.5} />
          {student?.has_unread_from_student && (
            <span aria-label="Unread message" className="absolute -right-0.5 -top-0.5 h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-sky-500 ring-2 ring-[#c3cace]" />
          )}
          {isOnline && (
            <span aria-label="Online on LSAT U" className="absolute -bottom-0.5 -right-0.5 h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 ring-2 ring-[#c3cace]" />
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            {/* The row opens the dossier; the chevron still expands inline for
                a quick access peek without leaving the roster. */}
            <Link
              to={`/teacher/students/${person.class_id}`}
              className="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-black underline-offset-4 hover:underline"
            >
              {person.display_name || 'No name'}
            </Link>
            <RoleBadge user={person} />
            {isOnline && (
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 border border-emerald-700/20 bg-emerald-500/10 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-emerald-800">
                <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-600" />
                Online
              </span>
            )}
            {student && student.assignments_total > 0 && (
              <span className="font-mono text-xs font-semibold tabular-nums text-black/50">
                {student.assignments_completed}/{student.assignments_total}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-black/45">
            <span className="truncate font-mono">{person.email}</span>
            <span>{lastActiveLabel}</span>
            <span
              className={cn(
                'inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-semibold',
                hasDrill ? 'text-emerald-700' : 'text-black/40',
              )}
            >
              <span className={cn('h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full', hasDrill ? 'bg-emerald-600' : 'bg-black/25')} />
              Drill {hasDrill ? 'on' : 'off'}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-end gap-1">
        {isAdmin ? (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold text-black/40">
            <Shield className="h-3 w-3" strokeWidth={1.5} />
            Full access
          </span>
        ) : hasAnyAccess ? (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setAllAccess(person, false)}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 border border-red-700/20 px-2 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-red-800 transition hover:bg-red-500/10"
          >
            <UserX className="h-3 w-3" strokeWidth={1.5} />
            Revoke All Access
          </button>
        ) : (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setAllAccess(person, true)}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 border border-emerald-700/20 px-2 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-800 transition hover:bg-emerald-500/10"
          >
            <UserCheck className="h-3 w-3" strokeWidth={1.5} />
            Grant Full Access
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

function PersonAccessPanel({
  person,
  setRole,
  resetOnboarding,
  setArchived,
  deleteStudent,
}: {
  person: PeopleRow;
  setRole: (userId: string, role: 'admin' | 'user') => void;
  resetOnboarding: (person: PeopleRow) => void;
  setArchived: (person: PeopleRow, archived: boolean) => void;
  deleteStudent: (person: PeopleRow) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-4 border-t border-black/10 px-8 py-4">
      <p className="text-xs leading-relaxed text-black/45">
        Bootcamp access is controlled by assignment, not here. Assign or archive a bootcamp from the
        Library to add or remove it from this student's path.
      </p>

      <div className="border-t border-black/10 pt-3">
        <div className="mb-2 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-black/35">Role Management</div>
        {person.role !== 'admin' ? (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setRole(person.class_id, 'admin')}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-amber-700/25 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-amber-800 transition hover:bg-amber-500/10"
          >
            <Crown className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={1.5} />
            Promote to Admin
          </button>
        ) : isPrimaryAdmin(person.email) ? (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-black/45" title="The primary admin account cannot be demoted.">
            <Shield className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={1.5} />
            Protected primary admin
          </span>
        ) : (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setRole(person.class_id, 'user')}
            className="border border-black/15 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-black/55 transition hover:bg-white/25 hover:text-black"
          >
            Demote to User
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="border-t border-black/10 pt-3">
        <div className="mb-2 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-black/35">Account</div>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => resetOnboarding(person)}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-black/15 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-black/55 transition hover:bg-white/25 hover:text-black"
        >
          <RotateCcw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={1.5} />
          Reset to a new account
        </button>
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-black/45">
          Clears their setup answers and name, re-locks access, and lets the intake
          run again. The login and their practice history survive.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Two ways off the list, deliberately far apart in weight. Archive is
          the one to reach for; delete is framed as the exception it is. */}
      <div className="border-t border-black/10 pt-3">
        <div className="mb-2 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-black/35">
          Remove from your list
        </div>
        {person.archived_at ? (
          <>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setArchived(person, false)}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-emerald-700/25 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-800 transition hover:bg-emerald-500/10"
            >
              <ArchiveRestore className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={1.5} />
              Bring back to the list
            </button>
            <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-black/45">
              Archived {new Date(person.archived_at).toLocaleDateString()}. Their
              records were kept. Restoring puts them back on your list with access
              still off, so you choose what to reopen.
            </p>
          </>
        ) : (
          <>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setArchived(person, true)}
              disabled={isPrimaryAdmin(person.email)}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-black/15 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-black/55 transition hover:bg-white/25 hover:text-black disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
            >
              <Archive className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={1.5} />
              Archive
            </button>
            <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-black/45">
              Turns off their access and takes them off your list. It lands on an
              open tab straight away.
              Nothing is deleted and you can bring them back at any time.
            </p>
          </>
        )}

        <div className="mt-4 border-t border-red-700/15 pt-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => deleteStudent(person)}
            disabled={isPrimaryAdmin(person.email)}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-red-700/25 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-red-800 transition hover:bg-red-500/10 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
          >
            <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={1.5} />
            Delete permanently
          </button>
          <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-black/45">
            {isPrimaryAdmin(person.email)
              ? 'The primary admin account cannot be deleted.'
              : 'Cannot be undone. Destroys their account and all their work. Your session records and their payment history are kept.'}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// One definition of "a paying student's access", used by the Stripe webhook and
// anything else that needs to open the product after a payment.
//
// A new account starts locked (see the profiles column defaults): they can see
// their classroom, finish the intake, message Joshua and book. Paying turns on
// the rest. Nothing here ever revokes — a refund is Joshua's call from People,
// not something a webhook should decide.

export const PAID_STUDENT_FLAGS = [
  "has_classroom_access",
  "has_practice_access",
  "has_drill_access",
  "has_bootcamp_access",
  "has_journal_access",
  "has_waj_access",
  "has_chat_access",
  "has_schedule_access",
  "has_activity_access",
] as const;

export type GrantResult =
  | { granted: true; class_id: string }
  | { granted: false; reason: "no_email" | "no_account" };

/** Opens the paid feature set for whoever owns `email`. */
export async function grantStudentAccess(
  admin: { auth: { admin: { listUsers: (opts: { perPage: number }) => Promise<any> } }; from: (t: string) => any },
  email: string | null | undefined,
): Promise<GrantResult> {
  if (!email) return { granted: false, reason: "no_email" };
  const target = email.trim().toLowerCase();

  const { data } = await admin.auth.admin.listUsers({ perPage: 1000 });
  const user = (data?.users ?? []).find(
    (u: { email?: string | null }) => (u.email ?? "").toLowerCase() === target,
  );
  // Someone can pay before they ever make an account. That is not an error:
  // Joshua grants them by hand once they sign up, and the payment record still
  // exists either way.
  if (!user) return { granted: false, reason: "no_account" };

  const updates: Record<string, boolean> = {};
  for (const flag of PAID_STUDENT_FLAGS) updates[flag] = true;

  const { error } = await admin.from("profiles").update(updates).eq("class_id", user.id);
  if (error) throw error;
  return { granted: true, class_id: user.id };
}

import { type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { Lock } from 'lucide-react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { useAuth } from '@/contexts/AuthContext';
import { useUserPermissions } from '@/hooks/useUserPermissions';
import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
import { bootcampIdFromRoute, classroomItemMatchesBootcamp } from '@/lib/bootcamps';

interface Props {
  /**
   * The route prefix for this bootcamp, e.g. "/bootcamp/principle-flaw" or
   * "/topics/abstract". Matched against either `metadata_json.bootcamp_id`
   * (slug, see `bootcampIdOverride`) or `source_id` on a classroom_items
   * row of type bootcamp_module.
   */
  routePath: string;
  /**
   * Override the slug extracted from `routePath`. Default behavior strips a
   * leading "/bootcamp/" and trailing path segments, which works for the
   * /bootcamp/* family. New surfaces under /topics/* should pass this
   * explicitly (e.g. routePath "/topics/abstract", bootcampIdOverride
   * "abstraction") so classroom_items rows assigned with the legacy slug
   * still resolve to the same gate.
   */
  bootcampIdOverride?: string;
  /**
   * Additional route paths to accept as `source_id` matches. Used when a
   * topic has moved (legacy /bootcamp/abstraction → /topics/abstract). The
   * current `routePath` is always matched.
   */
  legacyRoutePaths?: string[];
  /**
   * The student-facing display name, used only by the locked state.
   */
  title?: string;
  children: ReactNode;
}

/**
 * Per-student bootcamp gate. Admins always pass. Students need a
 * matching `classroom_items` row of type='bootcamp_module' assigned to
 * them. If no row exists, render a calm "Not assigned yet" surface.
 *
 * Direct URL hits go through this gate, so a student typing
 * /bootcamp/principle-flaw won't see anything they weren't assigned.
 */
export default function BootcampGate({
  routePath,
  bootcampIdOverride,
  legacyRoutePaths,
  title,
  children,
}: Props) {
  const { user, authReady } = useAuth();
  const { is_admin, loading: permsLoading } = useUserPermissions();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const bootcampId =
    bootcampIdOverride ??
    bootcampIdFromRoute(routePath);
  const explicitRoutePaths = [routePath, ...(legacyRoutePaths ?? [])];

  const { data: assigned, isLoading } = useQuery<boolean>({
    queryKey: ['bootcamp-gate', user?.id, routePath, bootcampId],
    enabled: !!user && !is_admin && !permsLoading,
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('classroom_items')
        .select('id, status, source_id, metadata_json')
        .eq('type', 'bootcamp_module')
        .eq('student_id', user!.id)
        .neq('status', 'archived');
      if (error) throw error;
      const rows = (data ?? []) as Array<{
        source_id: string | null;
        metadata_json: Record<string, unknown> | null;
      }>;
      return rows.some(
        (r) =>
          classroomItemMatchesBootcamp(r, bootcampId) ||
          (r.source_id
            ? explicitRoutePaths.some((path) => r.source_id === path || r.source_id?.startsWith(`${path}/`))
            : false),
      );
    },
  });

  if (!authReady || permsLoading) {
    return <div className="min-h-screen bg-zinc-950" />;
  }

  if (is_admin || assigned) {
    return <>{children}</>;
  }

  if (isLoading) {
    return <div className="min-h-screen bg-zinc-950" />;
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center px-6 py-12 bg-background">
      <div className="max-w-md text-center space-y-4">
        <div className="mx-auto w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-muted flex items-center justify-center">
          <Lock className="w-5 h-5 text-muted-foreground" strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Not assigned yet</h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground leading-relaxed">
            {title ? `${title} ` : 'This bootcamp '}
            isn't part of your current path. Joshua can add it from the Teacher Workspace.
          </p>
        </div>
        <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => navigate('/classroom')}>
          Back to Classroom
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
