src/components/drill/DrillLaunchGate.tsx
/**
 * Sits in front of the runner and turns a link into a run.
 *
 * It reads the launch out of the address bar, reads whatever row that launch
 * names, and hands the runner a Drill Run Spec. The address bar keeps the short
 * link it arrived with, so a refresh re-reads the row rather than replaying a
 * question list frozen into a URL.
 *
 * It has one other job. Eight buttons still hand a drill over in memory with
 * nothing in the address bar at all; those runs died on refresh. The gate names
 * what it was given and writes that into the address bar, once, as a `replace`
 * so Back still goes where the student came from.
 *
 * An older link — `?mode=type-drill&qids=…` — is read but never rewritten.
 * Everything such a link says is read in `parseDrillLaunch`, because there is
 * no second reader left to pick up what this one drops.
 */

import * as React from 'react';
import { useLocation, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';

import { useAuth } from '@/contexts/AuthContext';
import { useQuestionBank } from '@/contexts/QuestionBankContext';
import {
  DrillLaunchError,
  launchDrill,
  launchFromRouteState,
  launchNeedsResolution,
  parseDrillLaunch,
  type DrillHandoverState,
  type DrillLaunch,
  type PtLegRef,
} from '@/lib/drillLaunch';
import { resolveDrillLaunch } from '@/lib/drillLaunchResolve';
import { drillRunSpecFromLaunch, type DrillRunSpec } from '@/lib/drillRunSpec';
import { recordExposures } from '@/lib/questionExposure';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { DrillRunSpecProvider } from '@/components/drill/DrillRunSpecContext';

interface Props {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

const EMPTY_SPEC: DrillRunSpec = {};

/** Whatever the URL already said that a launch has no opinion about. */
function mergeUnclaimedParams(canonical: string, existing: URLSearchParams): string {
  const merged = new URLSearchParams(canonical);
  existing.forEach((value, key) => {
    if (!merged.has(key)) merged.set(key, value);
  });
  return merged.toString();
}

export default function DrillLaunchGate({ children }: Props) {
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const location = useLocation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { isLoading: questionBankLoading } = useQuestionBank();
  const [failure, setFailure] = React.useState<string | null>(null);
  const [resolved, setResolved] = React.useState<{ key: string; spec: DrillRunSpec } | null>(
    null,
  );

  const namedInUrl = !!searchParams.get('drill') || !!searchParams.get('mode');

  const { launch, parseError } = React.useMemo<{
    launch: DrillLaunch | null;
    parseError: string | null;
  }>(() => {
    try {
      const fromUrl = parseDrillLaunch(searchParams);
      if (fromUrl) return { launch: fromUrl, parseError: null };
    } catch (error) {
      return {
        launch: null,
        parseError:
          error instanceof DrillLaunchError
            ? error.message
            : 'That drill link could not be opened.',
      };
    }

    // Nothing in the address bar. What a button passed in memory, if anything.
    const routeState = location.state as (DrillHandoverState & { ptRun?: PtLegRef }) | null;
    const fromState = launchFromRouteState(routeState, routeState?.ptRun);
    if (!fromState) return { launch: null, parseError: null };
    // Some callers put the way back in the query string rather than in state
    // (`/drill?returnTo=/flagged`). Carry it onto the launch or the exit falls
    // back to Classroom Home.
    const queryReturnTo = searchParams.get('returnTo');
    if (queryReturnTo && !fromState.options?.returnTo) {
      return {
        launch: {
          ...fromState,
          options: { ...(fromState.options ?? {}), returnTo: queryReturnTo },
        },
        parseError: null,
      };
    }
    return { launch: fromState, parseError: null };
  }, [searchParams, location.state]);

  // Stable name for this run. The rewrite below produces the same key, which is
  // what stops the effect from resolving the same launch a second time.
  const launchKey = React.useMemo(() => (launch ? launchDrill(launch) : null), [launch]);

  const needsResolution = !!launch && launchNeedsResolution(launch);
  const waitsForQuestionBank =
    needsResolution && (launch.kind === 'bootcamp-day' || launch.kind === 'guided-drill');

  // The kinds that carry their own questions need no round trip, so they start
  // without a spinner.
  const syncSpec = React.useMemo(
    () => (launch && !needsResolution ? drillRunSpecFromLaunch(launch) : null),
    [launch, needsResolution],
  );

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!launch || !launchKey) return;
    if (!needsResolution) return;
    if (resolved?.key === launchKey) return;
    if (waitsForQuestionBank && questionBankLoading) return;
    let cancelled = false;

    const run = async () => {
      try {
        const qids = await resolveDrillLaunch(launch, user?.id);

        if (launch.kind === 'bootcamp-day' && user?.id && qids?.length) {
          void recordExposures(user.id, qids).catch((error) => {
            console.error('[DrillLaunchGate] recordExposures failed', error);
          });
        }

        if (cancelled) return;
        setResolved({ key: launchKey, spec: drillRunSpecFromLaunch(launch, qids) });
      } catch (error) {
        if (cancelled) return;
        setFailure(
          error instanceof DrillLaunchError
            ? error.message
            : 'That drill link could not be opened.',
        );
      }
    };

    void run();
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [
    launch,
    launchKey,
    needsResolution,
    resolved?.key,
    waitsForQuestionBank,
    questionBankLoading,
    user?.id,
  ]);

  // A handover with nothing in the address bar gets one written for it, so the
  // next refresh has something to rebuild the run from.
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (namedInUrl || !launchKey) return;
    navigate(
      { pathname: '/drill', search: mergeUnclaimedParams(launchKey, searchParams) },
      // `state: null` is load-bearing: it is what stops this effect from seeing
      // the same handover again and rewriting forever.
      { replace: true, state: null },
    );
  }, [namedInUrl, launchKey, searchParams, navigate]);

  const spec = syncSpec ?? (resolved?.key === launchKey ? resolved.spec : null);

  if (failure || parseError) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-background px-6">
        <div className="max-w-md text-center space-y-4">
          <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold text-foreground">This drill isn't available</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{failure ?? parseError}</p>
          <Button onClick={() => navigate('/classroom')}>Back to classroom</Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (launch && !spec) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-background">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden />
          Opening your drill…
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

export type DrillLaunchBody =
  /** The app picks. One continuous adaptive run per student. */
  | { kind: 'smart-drill' }
  /** A real PT section, optionally timed and optionally sealed. */
  | {
      kind: 'section';
      pt: number;
      section: number;
      /**
       * The clock this section runs on. Omitted means the standard 35. A
       * section can also be sat at 52.5, at 70, or with no clock at all, which
       * is how an accommodation is honoured, so this cannot be reduced to a
       * number of minutes.
       */
      timerMode?: Exclude<TimerMode, '35'>;
      /** Only meaningful with a `custom` timer. */
      minutes?: number;
      /**
       * Answer and Explanation are never rendered, for the whole run. A section
       * assigned as a measurement stops measuring the moment the student can
       * check question 3 before answering question 4. Set by whoever assigns
       * the section, never by the student.
       */
      sealed?: boolean;
      ptLeg?: PtLegRef;
    }
  /** A set the student saved. Named by its row, so editing the set is reflected. */
  | { kind: 'saved-set'; setId: string }
  /** A classroom assignment. Its row owns the question list. */
  | {
      kind: 'assignment';
      assignmentId: string;
      guidedSetId?: string;
      /** Only read when the assignment turns out to be one timed section. */
      timerMode?: Exclude<TimerMode, '35'>;
      /**
       * An assigned section is sealed by default: no Show Answer, no
       * Explanation, until it is scored. Only Joshua unseals one, at assign
       * time, and only on purpose.
       */
      revealAllowed?: boolean;
    }
  /** A guided drill, optionally the one handed over as an assignment. */
  | {
      kind: 'guided-drill';
      drillId?: string;
      assignmentId?: string;
      durationMinutes?: number;
    }
  /** A bootcamp module's practice set, named by its pool slug. */
  | { kind: 'practice-set'; slug: string; source?: string }
  /**
   * One day of a bootcamp, either its homework or its practice. The questions
   * are picked fresh each time from that day's filters, so the day and the part
   * are the run's real name — "Day 3 homework" — and the picked list is not.
   * Naming it this way means the link survives a change to how the picker
   * works, and the Run Record still holds the questions the student actually
   * got.
   *
   * Its Run Record is keyed by the day and part. The old question-list key is
   * read only as a migration fallback for runs started before that identity
   * existed.
   */
  | { kind: 'bootcamp-day'; day: number; part: 'homework' | 'practice' }
  /**
   * An ad-hoc list of specific questions: a question-finder jump, "redo this
   * one", a generated micro-drill. It has no row to point at, so the list is
   * the name.
   */
  | {
      kind: 'questions';
      qids: string[];
      /**
       * Set when the list arrived from an older link or an in-memory handover
       * that also named which stored row the run belongs to. Absent for a
       * plain "drill these questions" link.
       */
      carry?: RunCarry;
    }
  /**
   * "Six Flaw questions, medium." What the student asked for, rather than what
   * it happened to draw. Refresh returns the same questions because the Run
   * Record holds the queue; the filter only has to survive as the run's name.
   */
  | {
      kind: 'filter';
      count: number;
      qtypes?: string[];
      difficulties?: number[];
      pts?: number[];
      sections?: number[];
      qfeatures?: string[];
    };

import { type ReactNode } from "react";
import { Navigate, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";
import { useUserPermissions, type PermissionFlag } from "@/hooks/useUserPermissions";
import { authUrlWithReturnTo } from "@/lib/authRedirect";
import LockedModule from "@/components/LockedModule";

interface ProtectedRouteProps {
  /**
   * Optional feature flag, or a list of acceptable feature flags. Ignored when
   * `requireAdmin` is true.
   */
  flag?: PermissionFlag | readonly PermissionFlag[];
  /**
   * Allow any signed-in user through even when the feature flag is false.
   * Useful for core student workspace pages that should stay reachable while
   * their contents are still constrained by row-level data access.
   *
   * "Signed in is enough" — never "nobody needs to be signed in". A route that
   * should be public is not wrapped in this component at all.
   */
  allowSignedIn?: boolean;
  /**
   * When true, only admin users may render the children. Non-admin
   * users, regardless of feature flags, see LockedModule.
   */
  requireAdmin?: boolean;
  children: ReactNode;
}

const LOCAL_DEV_HOSTS = new Set(["localhost", "127.0.0.1", "::1"]);

/**
 * Opt-in only. A plain `npm run dev` now exercises the real permission path, so
 * a gating regression is visible locally instead of surfacing after deploy.
 * Set VITE_DEV_BYPASS_GATES=1 in .env.local to walk gated routes without the
 * matching flags.
 *
 * It relaxes *flags*, never authentication — the caller applies it only after
 * the signed-in check, so no configuration can make this component render to an
 * anonymous visitor.
 */
function isLocalDevAccessAllowed() {
  return (
    import.meta.env.DEV &&
    import.meta.env.VITE_DEV_BYPASS_GATES === "1" &&
    LOCAL_DEV_HOSTS.has(window.location.hostname)
  );
}

export default function ProtectedRoute({ flag, requireAdmin, allowSignedIn, children }: ProtectedRouteProps) {
  const { user, authReady } = useAuth();
  const permissions = useUserPermissions();
  const location = useLocation();

  // Auth first, and nothing before it. `useUserPermissions` reports
  // `loading: authReady && !!user && isPending`, which is *false* while auth is
  // still resolving — so a check on `!user` alone read "not yet known" as
  // "signed out" and bounced every cold deep link to /auth. Holding here until
  // auth is definitively resolved is what makes an assignment link survive.
  if (!authReady) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-background flex items-center justify-center">
        <div className="w-6 h-6 border-2 border-muted border-t-foreground rounded-full animate-spin" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  // Authentication is intrinsic to this component: every shape of it requires a
  // user. The check used to run only for `requireAdmin` or flagged routes, so a
  // wrapper with `allowSignedIn` and no flag fell through to the unconditional
  // render at the bottom and served protected content to anonymous visitors.
  if (!user) {
    const returnTo = `${location.pathname}${location.search}${location.hash}`;
    return <Navigate to={authUrlWithReturnTo(returnTo)} replace />;
  }

  // After the auth checks, never before: the dev bypass opens locked features
  // on a local machine, not the front door.

      _booking_id: bookingId,
      _stripe_checkout_session_id: session.id,
      _reason: "stripe_checkout_expired",
    });
    if (error && error.code !== "P0409") throw error;
    return json({ received: true });
  }

  if (event.type !== "checkout.session.completed") return json({ received: true });
  const session = event.data.object;

  // Any completed payment opens the product for that email, whatever it was
  // for. A single session, a package, a bootcamp: someone who has paid Joshua
  // money is a student, and the branches below still do their own work.
  if (session.payment_status === "paid") {
    try {
      const grant = await grantStudentAccess(
        supabase,
        session.customer_details?.email ?? session.customer_email ?? null,
      );
      console.log("access grant", { event: event.id, ...grant });
    } catch (err) {
      // Access is a convenience on top of the payment record. Failing to open
      // it must never make Stripe retry a payment that was already recorded.
      console.error("access grant failed", { event: event.id, err });
    }
  }

  if (session.metadata?.product === "single_session") {
    const bookingId = String(session.metadata?.booking_id || "");
    if (!bookingId) return json({ error: "invalid_product" }, 400);
    if (!session.id || typeof session.payment_intent !== "string") return json({ error: "missing_payment_facts" }, 400);
    const { data: booking, error: bookingError } = await supabase.from("single_session_bookings")
      .select("id,status,start_at,end_at,gcal_event_id,event_slug,email,quoted_amount_cents,quoted_currency")
      .eq("id", bookingId).maybeSingle();
    if (bookingError) throw bookingError;

export default function TestingCentre() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { manifest, isLoading, error } = useQuestionBank();
  const [door, setDoor] = React.useState<Door>(null);
  const { sections: rcSections, error: rcError } = useRcSections(door === "test");
  const [runs, setRuns] = React.useState<PracticeTestRun[]>([]);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    setRuns(listPracticeTestRuns().filter((r) => r.status === "in-progress"));
  }, []);

  const tests = React.useMemo(
    () => (manifest && rcSections ? buildPracticeTests(manifest, rcSections) : []),
    [manifest, rcSections],
  );

  const beginTest = (
    blueprint: PracticeTestBlueprint,
    pace: PracticeTestPaceId,
    sectionSeconds: number | null,
  ) => {
    const run = createPracticeTestRun(blueprint, pace, sectionSeconds);
    navigate(practiceTestPath(run.id));
  };

  const discard = (run: PracticeTestRun) => {
    deletePracticeTestRun(run.id);
    setRuns((current) => current.filter((r) => r.id !== run.id));
  };

  // A door's panel renders below the two door buttons, which on a laptop puts it
  // under the fold: the click looks like it did nothing, and the section type
  // picker (Logical Reasoning / Reading Comprehension) is never seen, so the door
  // reads as LR-only. Bring the panel to the student when it opens.
  //
  // Anchored on the doors rather than the panel. Scrolling the panel itself to
  // the top of the viewport over-corrected into the opposite failure: the panel
  // is taller than the viewport, so the page ran to its own scroll maximum and
  // took the room's name and the door that was just pressed with it. Measured at
  // 1280x900, .classroom-title landed at -234px and 78% of the door row was off
  // screen, at the moment a student commits to a timed section. Putting the door
  // row at the top keeps the choice that was just made on screen, and the panel
  // begins directly beneath it.
  const panelRef = React.useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const doorsRef = React.useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!door) return;
    const panel = panelRef.current;
    if (!panel) return;
    // Leave the page alone when the panel already starts on screen. The click
    // did not read as a no-op, and moving the page under a student who can
    // already see the result is its own small betrayal.
    const top = panel.getBoundingClientRect().top;
    if (top >= 0 && top <= window.innerHeight - PANEL_VISIBLE_MARGIN) return;
    const prefersReducedMotion = window.matchMedia?.(
      "(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)",
    ).matches;
    (doorsRef.current ?? panel).scrollIntoView({
      behavior: prefersReducedMotion ? "auto" : "smooth",
      block: "start",
    });
  }, [door]);

  return (
    <>
      <h1 className="classroom-title">Testing Centre</h1>
      <p className="testing-lede">
        Official LSAT material, timed like test day.
      </p>

      <ResumeList
        runs={runs}
        onResume={(run) => navigate(practiceTestPath(run.id))}
        onDiscard={discard}
      />

      {/* The doors carry a title and two supporting lines, and those used to be
          an h2 and a p. Neither is legal inside a button — its content model is
          phrasing only — and the h2s put two headings in the outline that a
          screen reader lands on as controls. They are spans that look the same.

          aria-expanded, not aria-pressed: these are not toggle buttons, they
          disclose a panel, and aria-controls is what says which panel. */}
      <div className="testing-doors" ref={doorsRef}>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="testing-door"
          aria-expanded={door === "section"}
          aria-controls={PANEL_ID}
          onClick={() => setDoor(door === "section" ? null : "section")}
        >
          <span className="testing-door-title">Take a section</span>
          {/* No clock here: the meta line below already carries it. */}
          <span className="testing-door-body">
            One real LR or RC section from an official PrepTest.
          </span>
          <span className="testing-door-meta">One section · 35 minutes</span>
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          className="testing-door"
          aria-expanded={door === "test"}
          aria-controls={PANEL_ID}
          onClick={() => setDoor(door === "test" ? null : "test")}
        >
          <span className="testing-door-title">Take a practice test</span>
          <span className="testing-door-body">
            Three scored sections from one PrepTest, in order, with a break.
          </span>
          <span className="testing-door-meta">Three sections · about 2 hours</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      <div id={PANEL_ID} ref={panelRef}>
        {door === "section" && (
          <div className="testing-panel">
            {isLoading && <Working>Loading questions…</Working>}
            {!isLoading && error && <p className="testing-note">{error}</p>}
            {!isLoading && !error && manifest && (
              <SectionSelector
                manifest={manifest}
                onStartSection={(config) =>
                  navigate("/drill", {
                    state: { mode: "full-section", config, returnTo: "/testing" },
                  })
                }
                onStartRcSection={(href) => navigate(href)}
                onCancel={() => setDoor(null)}
              />
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {door === "test" && (
          <>
            {(isLoading || (!rcSections && !rcError)) && (
              <div className="testing-panel">
                <Working>Working out which PrepTests can be sat end to end…</Working>
              </div>
            )}
            {!isLoading && (error || rcError) && (
              <div className="testing-panel">
                <p className="testing-note">{error || rcError}</p>
              </div>
            )}
            {!isLoading && !error && !rcError && manifest && rcSections && (
              <PracticeTestSelector tests={tests} onBegin={beginTest} />
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

export interface PtLegIdentity {
  runId: string;
  legIndex: number;
}

export interface LrLegLaunch {
  pathname: '/drill';
  state: {
    mode: 'full-section';
    config: FullSectionConfig;
    returnTo: string;
    practicingLabel: string;
    ptRun: PtLegIdentity;
  };
}

export function lrLegLaunch(
  run: PracticeTestRun,
  leg: PracticeTestLeg,
  legIndex: number,
): LrLegLaunch {
  return {
    pathname: '/drill',
    state: {
      mode: 'full-section',
      config: {
        pt: run.pt,
        section: leg.section,
        timer: {
          ...timerForRun(run),
          isPaused: false,
          elapsedMs: 0,
          startedAt: Date.now(),
        },
      },
      returnTo: practiceTestPath(run.id),
      practicingLabel: `Practice Test ${run.pt}`,
      ptRun: { runId: run.id, legIndex },
    },
  };
}

/**
 * The RC leg's link. `reveal=end_of_set` because a section is sat under test
 * conditions — nothing is revealed until the sitting is over, and inside a
 * practice test that is doubly true.
 */
export function rcLegHref(
  run: PracticeTestRun,
  leg: PracticeTestLeg,
  legIndex: number,
): string {
  const secondsPerPassage = (() => {
    // Same reading as the LR leg: the run's own resolved clock, so an
    // accommodated student's RC passages get their share of the longer section
    // rather than a share of 35 minutes.
    const sectionSeconds = practiceTestRunSectionSeconds(run);
    if (sectionSeconds === null) return null;
    const passages = leg.passageIds?.length || 4;
    return Math.round(sectionSeconds / passages);
  })();

  const params = new URLSearchParams({
    passages: (leg.passageIds ?? []).join(','),
    reveal: 'end_of_set',
    timing: secondsPerPassage === null ? 'untimed' : 'standard',
    start: '1',
    returnTo: practiceTestPath(run.id),
    ptRun: run.id,
    ptLeg: String(legIndex),
  });
  if (secondsPerPassage !== null && secondsPerPassage !== RC_STANDARD_SECONDS_PER_PASSAGE) {
    params.set('secondsPerPassage', String(secondsPerPassage));
  }
  return `/rc?${params.toString()}`;
}

The code now reads:\n--   /testing, /testing/test/:runId  -> has_practice_access   (was has_drill_access)\n--   /sets                           -> has_drill_access      (was has_practice_access)\n--   /classroom/ta/:assignmentId     -> has_ta_access         (was has_classroom_access)\n--   /journal*                       -> has_journal_access    (was journal OR waj)\n--\n-- This migration copies each student's current effective access into the\n-- column that now guards it, so nobody gains or loses the Testing Centre, TA\n-- worksheets or Wrong answers by the rewire.\n--\n-- The one place effective access moves is Saved Sets (/sets), which now follows\n-- Drill. A saved set launches /drill, so it was never usable without Drill.\n-- has_waj_access is left in place and no longer read.\n\nUPDATE public.profiles SET\n  has_practice_access = has_drill_access,\n  has_ta_access       = has_classroom_access,\n  has_journal_access  = has_journal_access OR has_waj_access;\n"}

{"file_path": "C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu-wt-revoke\\supabase\\migrations\\20260923010100_access_tiers_stage2_tier_column.sql", "content": "-- Access tiers, stage 2: the tier label.\n--\n-- A tier is a template. It sets the twelve switches; the switches stay what the\n-- app checks. access_tier only names which template the switches currently\n-- match, so the People page can say \"Tier 1\" without re-deriving it.\n--\n--   NULL    undecided: sits in the signup queue, floor access only\n--   tier_1  all twelve switches on\n--   tier_2  hand-picked switches\n--   tier_3  the floor (Classroom, Messages, Schedule) plus assigned work\n\nALTER TABLE public.profiles\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS access_tier text;\n\nALTER TABLE public.profiles\n  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS profiles_access_tier_check;\nALTER TABLE public.profiles\n  ADD CONSTRAINT profiles_access_tier_check\n  CHECK (access_tier IS NULL OR access_tier IN ('tier_1', 'tier_2', 'tier_3'));\n\nCOMMENT ON COLUMN public.profiles.access_tier IS\n  'Access template the switches match. NULL = undecided (signup queue). Label only: the has_*_access switches are what the app enforces.';\n\n-- The tier is an admin act, like the switches it names.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_profile_admin_fields()\n RETURNS trigger\n LANGUAGE plpgsql\n SET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nBEGIN\n  IF auth.uid() IS NULL OR public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin') THEN\n    RETURN NEW;\n  END IF;\n\n  IF NEW.has_bootcamp_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_bootcamp_access  OR\n     NEW.has_classroom_access IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_classroom_access OR\n     NEW.has_analytics_access IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_analytics_access OR\n     NEW.has_schedule_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_schedule_access  OR\n     NEW.has_practice_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_practice_access  OR\n     NEW.has_drill_access     IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_drill_access     OR\n     NEW.has_waj_access       IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_waj_access       OR\n     NEW.has_journal_access   IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_journal_access   OR\n     NEW.has_flagged_access   IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_flagged_access   OR\n     NEW.has_chat_access      IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_chat_access      OR\n     NEW.has_export_access    IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_export_access    OR\n     NEW.has_ta_access        IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_ta_access        OR\n     NEW.has_activity_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_activity_access  OR\n     NEW.access_tier          IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.access_tier          OR\n     NEW.archived_at          IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.archived_at          OR\n     NEW.archived_reason      IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.archived_reason      OR\n     NEW.proficiency_level    IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.proficiency_level    OR\n     NEW.class_id             IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.class_id             THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'profiles: access flags, access tier, archive state, proficiency_level and class_id are admin-controlled';\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND;\n$function$;\n\n-- Drift: a Tier 1 or Tier 3 label only holds while the switches match it.\n-- Flip one switch away from the template and the label becomes Tier 2. Label\n-- only, never access: the switch that was flipped is the change, this just\n-- stops the page lying about it. Lives in the database so every write path,\n-- including a bulk update, keeps the label honest.\n--\n-- A write that sets access_tier itself (set_tier, archive, reset) is trusted\n-- and left alone, which is what lets set_tier move a row between templates.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.profiles_access_tier_drift()\n RETURNS trigger\n LANGUAGE plpgsql\n SET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE\n  all_on boolean;\n  floor_only boolean;\nBEGIN\n  IF NEW.access_tier IS NULL OR NEW.access_tier = 'tier_2' THEN\n    RETURN NEW;\n  END IF;\n  IF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND NEW.access_tier IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.access_tier THEN\n    RETURN NEW;\n  END IF;\n\n  all_on :=\n    NEW.has_drill_access AND NEW.has_practice_access AND NEW.has_activity_access AND\n    NEW.has_bootcamp_access AND NEW.has_journal_access AND NEW.has_flagged_access AND\n    NEW.has_analytics_access AND NEW.has_export_access AND NEW.has_classroom_access AND\n    NEW.has_chat_access AND NEW.has_schedule_access AND NEW.has_ta_access;\n\n  floor_only :=\n    NEW.has_classroom_access AND NEW.has_chat_access AND NEW.has_schedule_access AND NOT (\n      NEW.has_drill_access OR NEW.has_practice_access OR NEW.has_activity_access OR\n      NEW.has_bootcamp_access OR NEW.has_journal_access OR NEW.has_flagged_access OR\n      NEW.has_analytics_access OR NEW.has_export_access OR NEW.has_ta_access\n    );\n\n  IF NEW.access_tier = 'tier_1' AND NOT all_on THEN\n    NEW.access_tier := 'tier_2';\n  ELSIF NEW.access_tier = 'tier_3' AND NOT floor_only THEN\n    NEW.access_tier := 'tier_2';\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND;\n$function$;\n\nDROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS profiles_access_tier_drift_trg ON public.profiles;\nCREATE TRIGGER profiles_access_tier_drift_trg\n  BEFORE UPDATE ON public.profiles\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.profiles_access_tier_drift();\n"}

{"file_path": "C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu-wt-revoke\\supabase\\migrations\\20260923010200_access_tiers_stage3_assign_tiers.sql", "content": "-- Access tiers, stage 3: give every existing account a tier, and start new\n-- signups at the floor.\n--\n-- Runs after stage 1, so the twelve switches already mean what they say.\n-- Admins are not students, but they are all-on, so they land on Tier 1; the\n-- label does nothing for an admin, who bypasses every gate.\n\n-- Everyone with all twelve on.\nUPDATE public.profiles SET access_tier = 'tier_1'\nWHERE access_tier IS NULL AND archived_at IS NULL\n  AND has_drill_access AND has_practice_access AND has_activity_access AND\n      has_bootcamp_access AND has_journal_access AND has_flagged_access AND\n      has_analytics_access AND has_export_access AND has_classroom_access AND\n      has_chat_access AND has_schedule_access AND has_ta_access;\n\n-- Classroom and nothing else: the revoked group. They become Tier 3, and Tier 3\n-- includes Messages and Schedule, so this is the one deliberate widening in the\n-- whole change (decision 17 of the 2026-09-22 plan): they can reach Joshua and\n-- book again. TA worksheets follow Classroom after stage 1, so they are cleared\n-- here with the rest of the non-floor switches.\nUPDATE public.profiles SET\n  access_tier = 'tier_3',\n  has_chat_access = true,\n  has_schedule_access = true,\n  has_ta_access = false\nWHERE access_tier IS NULL AND archived_at IS NULL\n  AND has_classroom_access\n  AND NOT (has_drill_access OR has_practice_access OR has_activity_access OR\n           has_bootcamp_access OR has_journal_access OR has_flagged_access OR\n           has_analytics_access OR has_export_access);\n\n-- Everyone else keeps exactly the switches they have, labelled Tier 2.\nUPDATE public.profiles SET access_tier = 'tier_2'\nWHERE access_tier IS NULL AND archived_at IS NULL;\n\n-- New signups: the floor, undecided. has_chat_access and has_schedule_access\n-- already default to true and every other switch to false; Classroom joins the\n-- floor so a new student has somewhere to stand while they wait in the queue.\nALTER TABLE public.profiles\n  ALTER COLUMN has_classroom_access SET DEFAULT true,\n  ALTER COLUMN access_tier          SET DEFAULT NULL;\n"}

NOT NULL,\n  assigned_by uuid,\n  assigned_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  unlock_at   timestamptz,\n  status      text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'assigned'\n              CHECK (status IN ('assigned', 'completed', 'archived')),\n  UNIQUE (student_id, pt)\n);\n\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS practice_test_assignments_student_idx\n  ON public.practice_test_assignments (student_id);\n\nALTER TABLE public.practice_test_assignments ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS \"Admins manage practice test assignments\" ON public.practice_test_assignments;\nCREATE POLICY \"Admins manage practice test assignments\"\n  ON public.practice_test_assignments FOR ALL\n  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'))\n  WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'));\n\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS \"Students read own practice test assignments\" ON public.practice_test_assignments;\nCREATE POLICY \"Students read own practice test assignments\"\n  ON public.practice_test_assignments FOR SELECT\n  USING (\n    student_id = auth.uid()\n    AND status <> 'archived'\n    AND (unlock_at IS NULL OR unlock_at <= now())\n  );\n\n-- True when this user may start a sitting of this PrepTest: the Practice tests\n-- switch, an admin, or a live assignment for exactly this PT.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.can_sit_practice_test(_user uuid, _pt integer)\n RETURNS boolean\n LANGUAGE sql\n STABLE\n SECURITY DEFINER\n SET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\n  SELECT\n    public.has_role(_user, 'admin')\n    OR EXISTS (\n      SELECT 1 FROM public.profiles\n      WHERE class_id = _user::text AND has_practice_access\n    )\n    OR EXISTS (\n      SELECT 1 FROM public.practice_test_assignments\n      WHERE student_id = _user AND pt = _pt AND status <> 'archived'\n        AND (unlock_at IS NULL OR unlock_at <= now())\n    );\n$function$;\n\n-- The API cannot be used to start a sitting the student was not given.\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS pt_runs_owner_insert ON public.practice_test_runs;\nCREATE POLICY pt_runs_owner_insert\n  ON public.practice_test_runs FOR INSERT\n  WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid() AND public.can_sit_practice_test(auth.uid(), pt));\n"}

The tier column only names the template the\n// switches currently match; the database relabels Tier 1 or Tier 3 as Tier 2\n// the moment a switch moves away from its template.\n\nexport type AccessTier = 'tier_1' | 'tier_2' | 'tier_3';\n\n/** Classroom home, Messages, Schedule: what every account keeps. */\nexport const FLOOR_FLAG_KEYS = ['has_classroom_access', 'has_chat_access', 'has_schedule_access'] as const;\n\nexport const ACCESS_TIERS: { id: AccessTier; label: string; description: string }[] = [\n  { id: 'tier_1', label: 'Tier 1', description: 'Everything on.' },\n  { id: 'tier_2', label: 'Tier 2', description: 'You choose each feature.' },\n  { id: 'tier_3', label: 'Tier 3', description: 'Classroom, Messages and Schedule, plus what you assign.' },\n];\n\nexport const TIER_LABELS: Record<AccessTier, string> = {\n  tier_1: 'Tier 1',\n  tier_2: 'Tier 2',\n  tier_3: 'Tier 3',\n};\n\n/** The switches a tier writes. Tier 2 writes nothing: it keeps what is there. */\nexport function tierFlags(tier: AccessTier): Record<string, boolean> | null {\n  if (tier === 'tier_2') return null;\n  const out: Record<string, boolean> = {};\n  for (const key of CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS) {\n    out[key] = tier === 'tier_1' || (FLOOR_FLAG_KEYS as readonly string[]).includes(key);\n  }\n  return out;\n}\nEOF\npython - <<'EOF'\np='src/lib/adminUsers.ts'\ns=open(p,encoding='utf-8').read()\ns=s.replace(\"\"\"  archived_reason: string | null;\n  has_bootcamp_access\"\"\",\"\"\"  archived_reason: string | null;\n  /** Which access template the switches match. null = undecided, in the signup queue. */\n  access_tier: 'tier_1' | 'tier_2' | 'tier_3' | null;\n  has_bootcamp_access\"\"\",1)\nopen(p,'w',encoding='utf-8').write(s)\nEOF\ngrep -n \"access_tier\" src/lib/adminUsers.ts | head -3", "description": "Add tier definitions to admin users lib"}

Assigning one\n * activity must open that activity, not the Activity Centre; assigning one\n * PrepTest must open that sitting, not the Testing Centre. So an assignment\n * authorises its own runner: this gate lets the child render when the feature\n * switch is on OR when `authorize` proves the signed-in student owns the\n * specific thing in the route. Otherwise it falls through to the ordinary\n * ProtectedRoute, which shows the locked state.\n *\n * Same shape as BootcampGate, which has done this for bootcamps all along.\n * Route-level only: RLS already scopes every assignment table by student_id,\n * so this decides what a student is shown, not what they can read.\n */\nexport function AssignmentGate({\n  flag,\n  authKey,\n  authorize,\n  children,\n}: {\n  flag: PermissionFlag | readonly PermissionFlag[];\n  /** Stable name for the thing being authorised, for caching. null = nothing to authorise. */\n  authKey: string | null;\n  authorize: (userId: string) => Promise<boolean>;\n  children: ReactNode;\n}) {\n  const { user, authReady } = useAuth();\n  const permissions = useUserPermissions();\n  const flags = (Array.isArray(flag) ? flag : [flag]) as PermissionFlag[];\n  const flagOpen = permissions.is_admin || flags.some((f) => Boolean(permissions[f]));\n\n  const { data: assigned, isLoading } = useQuery<boolean>({\n    queryKey: [\"assignment-gate\", user?.id, authKey],\n    enabled: authReady && !!user && !permissions.loading && !flagOpen && !!authKey,\n    queryFn: () => authorize(user!.id),\n    staleTime: 60_000,\n  });\n\n  const waiting =\n    authReady && !!user && !permissions.loading && !flagOpen && !!authKey && isLoading;\n  if (waiting) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-background flex items-center justify-center\">\n        <div className=\"w-6 h-6 border-2 border-muted border-t-foreground rounded-full animate-spin\" />\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (!flagOpen && assigned) return <>{children}</>;\n  return <ProtectedRoute flag={flag}>{children}</ProtectedRoute>;\n}\n\n// ── What each runner accepts as proof ──────────────────────────────────────\n\nasync function ownsRow(table: string, id: string, userId: string): Promise<boolean> {\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\n    .from(table as never)\n    .select(\"id\")\n    .eq(\"id\", id)\n    .eq(\"student_id\", userId)\n    .maybeSingle();\n  if (error) return false;\n  return !!data;\n}\n\n/** A live practice-test assignment for exactly this PrepTest. */\nexport async function hasPracticeTestAssignment(userId: string, pt?: number): Promise<boolean> {\n  let query = supabase\n    .from(\"practice_test_assignments\" as never)\n    .select(\"id\")\n    .eq(\"student_id\", userId)\n    .neq(\"status\", \"archived\");\n  if (typeof pt === \"number\") query = query.eq(\"pt\", pt);\n  const { data, error } = await query.limit(1);\n  if (error) return false;\n  return (data ?? []).length > 0;\n}\n\nasync function hasAssignedBootcamp(userId: string): Promise<boolean> {\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\n    .from(\"classroom_items\")\n    .select(\"id\")\n    .eq(\"student_id\", userId)\n    .eq(\"type\", \"bootcamp_module\")\n    .neq(\"status\", \"archived\")\n    .limit(1);\n  if (error) return false;\n  return (data ?? []).length > 0;\n}\n\n/** The PrepTest a sitting leg belongs to, read from the run on this device. */\nfunction ptOfRun(runId: string | null | undefined): number | null {\n  if (!runId) return null;\n  return loadPracticeTestRun(runId)?.pt ?? null;\n}\n\n// ── Route wrappers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/** /activities/:assignmentId */\nexport function ActivityAssignmentGate({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\n  const { assignmentId } = useParams<{ assignmentId: string }>();\n  return (\n    <AssignmentGate\n      flag=\"has_activity_access\"\n      authKey={assignmentId ? `activity:${assignmentId}` : null}\n      authorize={(uid) => ownsRow(\"activity_assignments\", assignmentId!, uid)}\n    >\n      {children}\n    </AssignmentGate>\n  );\n}\n\n/** /classroom/ta/:assignmentId */\nexport function TAAssignmentGate({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\n  const { assignmentId } = useParams<{ assignmentId: string }>();\n  return (\n    <AssignmentGate\n      flag=\"has_ta_access\"\n      authKey={assignmentId ? `ta:${assignmentId}` : null}\n      authorize={(uid) => ownsRow(\"ta_assignments\", assignmentId!, uid)}\n    >\n      {children}\n    </AssignmentGate>\n  );\n}\n\n/** /testing: the Testing Centre lists only assigned PrepTests without the switch. */\nexport function TestingCentreGate({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\n  return (\n    <AssignmentGate\n      flag=\"has_practice_access\"\n      authKey=\"testing:any\"\n      authorize={(uid) => hasPracticeTestAssignment(uid)}\n    >\n      {children}\n    </AssignmentGate>\n  );\n}\n\n/** /testing/test/:runId */\nexport function PracticeTestRunGate({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\n  const { runId } = useParams<{ runId: string }>();\n  const pt = ptOfRun(runId);\n  return (\n    <AssignmentGate\n      flag=\"has_practice_access\"\n      authKey={pt !== null ? `pt:${pt}` : null}\n      authorize={(uid) => hasPracticeTestAssignment(uid, pt!)}\n    >\n      {children}\n    </AssignmentGate>\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * What an assignment-launched drill run is authorised by, if anything. Pure so\n * it can be tested without a router.\n */\nexport type DrillAuthorisation =\n  | { kind: \"homework\"; id: string }\n  | { kind: \"guided\"; id: string }\n  | { kind: \"practice-test\"; pt: number }\n  | { kind: \"bootcamp\" }\n  | null;\n\nexport function drillAuthorisation(\n  launch: DrillLaunch | null,\n  ptRunId: string | null,\n  ptOf: (runId: string) => number | null = ptOfRun as (runId: string) => number | null,\n): DrillAuthorisation {\n  if (ptRunId) {\n    const pt = ptOf(ptRunId);\n    if (pt !== null) return { kind: \"practice-test\", pt };\n  }\n  if (!launch) return null;\n  switch (launch.kind) {\n    case \"assignment\":\n      return { kind: \"homework\", id: launch.assignmentId };\n    case \"guided-drill\":\n      return launch.assignmentId ? { kind: \"guided\", id: launch.assignmentId } : null;\n    case \"bootcamp-day\":\n    case \"practice-set\":\n      return { kind: \"bootcamp\" };\n    case \"section\":\n      if (launch.ptLeg?.runId) {\n        const pt = ptOf(launch.ptLeg.runId);\n        if (pt !== null) return { kind: \"practice-test\", pt };\n      }\n      return null;\n    default:\n      return null;\n  }\n}\n\nasync function authoriseDrill(auth: NonNullable<DrillAuthorisation>, uid: string): Promise<boolean> {\n  switch (auth.kind) {\n    case \"homework\":\n      return ownsRow(\"homework_assignments\", auth.id, uid);\n    case \"guided\":\n      return ownsRow(\"guided_drill_assignments\", auth.id, uid);\n    case \"practice-test\":\n      return hasPracticeTestAssignment(uid, auth.pt);\n    case \"bootcamp\":\n      return hasAssignedBootcamp(uid);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction authKeyOf(auth: DrillAuthorisation): string | null {\n  if (!auth) return null;\n  switch (auth.kind) {\n    case \"homework\":\n    case \"guided\":\n      return `${auth.kind}:${auth.id}`;\n    case \"practice-test\":\n      return `pt:${auth.pt}`;\n    case \"bootcamp\":\n      return \"bootcamp:any\";\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * /drill, /rc and /rc/drill. The runner is shared, so the Drill switch opens it\n * for anything, and an assignment opens it for that one run: a homework set, an\n * assigned guided drill, an assigned bootcamp's days, or a leg of an assigned\n * PrepTest sitting.\n */\nexport function DrillAssignmentGate({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\n  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();\n  const location = useLocation();\n  const state = location.state as { ptRun?: { runId?: string } } | null;\n\n  let launch: DrillLaunch | null = null;\n  try {\n    launch = parseDrillLaunch(searchParams);\n  } catch {\n    launch = null;\n  }\n  const ptRunId = state?.ptRun?.runId ?? searchParams.get(\"ptRun\");\n  const auth = drillAuthorisation(launch, ptRunId);\n\n  return (\n    <AssignmentGate\n      flag=\"has_drill_access\"\n      authKey={authKeyOf(auth)}\n      authorize={(uid) => (auth ? authoriseDrill(auth, uid) : Promise.resolve(false))}\n    >\n      {children}\n    </AssignmentGate>\n  );\n}\n"}

import { useLocation, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useUserPermissions } from "@/hooks/useUserPermissions";

export type ReviewSection = "journal" | "flagged";

export function reviewDefaultPath(permissions: {
  loading?: boolean;
  is_admin: boolean;
  has_journal_access: boolean;
  has_waj_access: boolean;
  has_flagged_access: boolean;
}) {
  const canOpenJournal =
    permissions.loading ||
    permissions.is_admin ||
    permissions.has_journal_access ||
    permissions.has_waj_access;
  return canOpenJournal ? "/journal" : "/flagged";
}

export function ReviewTabs() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { pathname } = useLocation();

    permissions.is_admin ||
    permissions.has_journal_access ||
    permissions.has_waj_access;

    <div className="classroom-account-heading classroom-review-heading">
      <h1 className="classroom-title">Review</h1>
      <div
        className="classroom-account-tabs"

const ASSIGNMENT_SOURCE_TABLES = new Set(['homework_assignments']);

function openableAssignmentId(item: ClassroomItem): string | null {
  if (!item.source_id) return null;
  if (item.source_table && !ASSIGNMENT_SOURCE_TABLES.has(item.source_table)) return null;
  return item.source_id;
}

/**
 * Resolve a classroom_items row to the right student-facing route.
 * Returns the path to navigate to, or null if the item is not actionable.
 */
export function resolveItemRoute(item: ClassroomItem): string | null {
  switch (item.type) {
    case 'drill':
      if (item.source_table === 'guided_drill_assignments' && item.source_id) {
        return `/guided/${encodeURIComponent(item.source_id)}?returnTo=${encodeURIComponent('/classroom')}`;
      }
      if (item.source_id) return `/drill?assignmentId=${encodeURIComponent(item.source_id)}`;
      return '/drill';
    case 'homework': {
      const assignmentId = openableAssignmentId(item);
      if (assignmentId) return `/classroom/${encodeURIComponent(assignmentId)}`;
      if (isSelfContainedItem(item)) return `/classroom/item/${encodeURIComponent(item.id)}`;
      return '/classroom';
    }
    case 'activity':
      // source_id is the activity_assignments row id; source_table='activity_assignments'.
      if (item.source_id) return `/activities/${encodeURIComponent(item.source_id)}`;
      return '/activities';
    case 'bootcamp_module':
      if (item.source_id) return item.source_id; // expected to be a /bootcamp/* path
      return '/bootcamps';
    case 'session_recap':
    case 'smartboard_recap':
    case 'announcement':
    case 'note':
      return `/classroom/item/${encodeURIComponent(item.id)}`;
    default:
      return `/classroom/item/${encodeURIComponent(item.id)}`;
  }
}
 * collide.
 */

/** The carry as the canonical grammar writes it. Mirror of `launchDrill`. */
function readCarry(p: URLSearchParams): RunCarry | undefined {
  const day = positiveNumber(p.get('day'));
  const part = readPart(p.get('part'));
  return buildCarry({
    mode: (p.get('runMode') as DrillMode | null) ?? undefined,
    sealed: p.get('runSealed') === '1',
    isGuidedDrill: p.get('guided') === '1',
    guidedDurationMinutes: positiveNumber(p.get('guidedMinutes')),
    assignmentId: p.get('assignment') || undefined,
    guidedSetId: p.get('guidedSet') || undefined,
    guidedDrillId: p.get('guidedDrill') || undefined,
    guidedAssignmentId: p.get('guidedAssignment') || undefined,
    practiceSource: p.get('source') || undefined,
    practiceSlug: p.get('slug') || undefined,
    resumeIdentity: day && part ? { kind: 'bootcamp-day', day, part } : undefined,
    ptLeg: readPtLeg(p.get('runId'), p.get('leg')),
  });
}

/** The same carry as the older grammar wrote it, under its own param names. */
function readLegacyCarry(p: URLSearchParams, mode: DrillMode): RunCarry | undefined {
  const day = positiveNumber(p.get('bootcampDay'));
  const part = readPart(p.get('bootcampPart'));
  return buildCarry({
    mode,
    sealed: p.get('sealed') === '1',
    isGuidedDrill: p.get('guided') === '1' || !!p.get('guidedAssignmentId'),
    guidedDurationMinutes: positiveNumber(p.get('guidedDurationMinutes')),
    assignmentId: p.get('assignmentId') || undefined,
    guidedSetId: p.get('guidedSetId') || undefined,
    guidedDrillId: p.get('guidedDrillId') || undefined,
    guidedAssignmentId: p.get('guidedAssignmentId') || undefined,
    practiceSource: p.get('source') || undefined,
    practiceSlug: p.get('practiceSlug') || undefined,
    resumeIdentity: day && part ? { kind: 'bootcamp-day', day, part } : undefined,
    ptLeg: readPtLeg(p.get('runId'), p.get('leg')),
  });
}

function readOptions(p: URLSearchParams): DrillLaunchOptions | undefined {
  const options: DrillLaunchOptions = {};
  const returnTo = safeReturnTo(p.get('returnTo'));
  if (returnTo) options.returnTo = returnTo;

    case 'saved-set': {
      const setId = p.get('set');
      if (!setId) throw new DrillLaunchError('a saved-set launch needs a set id');
      return { kind, setId, options };
    }

    case 'assignment': {
      const assignmentId = p.get('assignment');
      if (!assignmentId) {
        throw new DrillLaunchError('an assignment launch needs an assignment id');
      }
      return {
        kind,
        assignmentId,
        guidedSetId: p.get('guidedSet') || undefined,
        timerMode: readTimerMode(p.get('timer')),
        revealAllowed: p.get('reveal') === '1' || undefined,
        options,
      };
    }

      const drillId = p.get('guidedDrill') || undefined;
      const assignmentId = p.get('assignment') || undefined;

import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { format, isSameDay, parseISO } from 'date-fns';
import { DeskChatPanel, type DeskChatInsert } from '../desk/DeskChatPanel';
import { DeskTodoRail } from '../desk/DeskTodoRail';
import { useDeskTaskQueue } from '../desk/useDeskTaskQueue';
import type { CalendarTodoItem } from '../desk/teacherTodoTypes';

/**
 * A to-do `openPath` may still be written in the old inbox form
 * (`/inbox?pane=gmail&t=…`). Fold it onto the Inbox room, dropping only `pane`
 * so the thread and every other param survive. This is the one place that
 * knows about the legacy shape; no surface reads `?ws=` any more.
 */
export function teacherRoomPathForSource(path: string) {
  if (!path.startsWith('/inbox')) return path;
  const url = new URL(path, 'http://lsatu.local');
  const next = new URLSearchParams();
  url.searchParams.forEach((value, key) => {
    if (key === 'pane') return;
    next.set(key, value);
  });
  const query = next.toString();
  return `/teacher/inbox${query ? `?${query}` : ''}`;
}

/**
 * Today: the work queue, not a chat window (spec 5.1). The queue is the main
 * column; today's sessions and the counts sit above it.
 *
 * Every number here is counted off the queue that is rendered directly below
 * it, so a count can never claim work the list does not show. While the
 * sources are still loading the counts are withheld rather than shown as zero.
 */
export default function DeskTodaySurface() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [chatInsert, setChatInsert] = useState<DeskChatInsert | null>(null);
  const { localPreview, todoQueue, isLoading } = useDeskTaskQueue();

  const items = todoQueue.items;

  const todaySessions = useMemo(
    () =>
      items.filter(
        (item): item is CalendarTodoItem =>
          item.sourceKind === 'calendar' && isSameDay(parseISO(item.startAt), new Date()),
      ),
    [items],
  );

  const counts = useMemo(
    () => ({
      email: items.filter((item) => item.priorityBand === 'email').length,
      calendar: items.filter((item) => item.priorityBand === 'calendar').length,
      work: items.filter((item) => item.priorityBand === 'work').length,
    }),
    [items],
  );

  // A plain stack, not a flex-1 column: the queue sizes to its own content and
  // `main` does the scrolling. Letting the sections flex-shrink collapsed the
  // list and painted its cards over the section below it.
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 px-5 py-6 sm:px-6">
      <section className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_260px]">
        <div className="border border-black/12 bg-white/25 px-4 py-3.5">
          <div className="mb-2.5 text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-black/40">
            Today&rsquo;s sessions
          </div>
          {isLoading ? (
            <p className="text-[13px] text-black/45">Loading</p>
          ) : todaySessions.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-[13px] text-black/45">Nothing on the calendar for today.</p>
          ) : (
            <ul className="space-y-1.5">
              {todaySessions.map((session) => (
                <li key={session.id} className="flex items-baseline gap-3 text-[13px]">
                  <span className="w-[72px] shrink-0 tabular-nums text-black/50">
                    {format(parseISO(session.startAt), 'h:mm a')}
                  </span>
                  <span className="truncate">{session.title}</span>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="border border-black/12 bg-white/25 px-4 py-3.5">
          <div className="mb-2.5 text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-black/40">Waiting</div>
          <dl className="space-y-1.5 text-[13px]">
            {[
              { label: 'Email', value: counts.email },
              { label: 'Calendar', value: counts.calendar },
              { label: 'Student work', value: counts.work },
            ].map(({ label, value }) => (
              <div key={label} className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-3">
                <dt className="text-black/50">{label}</dt>
                <dd className="tabular-nums">{isLoading ? '—' : value}</dd>
              </div>
            ))}
          </dl>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section>
        <DeskTodoRail
          items={items}
          loading={isLoading}
          onOpen={(path) => navigate(teacherRoomPathForSource(path))}
          onInsertInChat={(text) => setChatInsert({ id: Date.now(), text })}
        />
      </section>

      {/* Interim home for the assistant. It moves into the shell's AI panel in
          Phase 3; until then Today keeps the composer so the queue's
          "draft this" action still has somewhere to land. */}
      <section className="flex h-[520px] shrink-0 flex-col border-t border-black/10 pt-5">
        <DeskChatPanel
          localPreview={localPreview}
          insert={chatInsert}
          onInsertConsumed={() => setChatInsert(null)}
        />
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

TIER_LABELS[tier] : 'No tier'}\n    </span>\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * The tier picker and, under it, the twelve switches. []) as Assigned[];\n    },\n  });\n\n  const call = async (action: 'assign_practice_test' | 'unassign_practice_test', value: number) => {\n    setBusy(true);\n    try {\n      const res = await toast.success(\n        action === 'assign_practice_test'\n          ? `PrepTest ${value} assigned to ${who}`\n          : `PrepTest ${value} taken off ${who}'s work`,\n      );\n      setPt('');\n      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: key });\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error('practice test assignment failed', { action, classId, value, error });\n      toast.error('That did not save');\n    } finally {\n      setBusy(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const ptNumber = Number(pt);\n  const valid = Number.isInteger(ptNumber) && ptNumber > 0;\n\n  return (\n    <div>\n      <div onSubmit={(event) => {\n          event.preventDefault();\n          if (valid) void call('assign_practice_test', ptNumber);\n        }}\n      >\n        <label className=\"sr-only\" htmlFor={`assign-pt-${classId}`}>PrepTest number</label>\n        <input\n          id={`assign-pt-${classId}`}\n          inputMode=\"numeric\"\n          placeholder=\"PT number\"\n          value={pt}\n          onChange={(event) => setPt(event.target.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, ''))}\n          className=\"h-8 w-28 border border-black/15 bg-white/40 px-2 text-xs\"\n        />\n        <button\n          type=\"submit\"\n          disabled={!valid || busy}\n          className=\"h-8 border border-black/15 px-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-black/60 transition hover:bg-white/25 hover:text-black disabled:opacity-40\"\n        >\n          Assign sitting\n        </button>\n      </form>\n      {assigned.length > 0 && (\n        <ul className=\"mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5\">\n          {assigned.map((row) => (\n            <li\n              key={row.pt}\n              className=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1 border border-black/15 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-black/60\"\n            >\n              PT {row.pt}\n              {row.status === 'completed' && <span className=\"text-emerald-700\">done</span>}\n              <button\n                type=\"button\"\n                disabled={busy}\n                onClick={() => call('unassign_practice_test', row.pt)}\n                aria-label={`Unassign PrepTest ${row.pt}`}\n                className=\"text-black/40 hover:text-black disabled:opacity-40\"\n              >\n                <X className=\"h-3 w-3\" strokeWidth={2} />\n              </button>\n            </li>\n          ))}\n        </ul>\n      )}\n      <p className=\"mt-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-black/45\">\n        Opens that one full sitting, even without the Practice tests switch.\n      </p>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"}

import { fireEvent, render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { MemoryRouter } from 'react-router-dom';
import { DeskPeopleSurface } from './DeskPeopleSurface';

const { invokeMock, getSessionMock, toastErrorMock } = vi.hoisted(() => ({
  invokeMock: vi.fn(),
  getSessionMock: vi.fn(),
  toastErrorMock: vi.fn(),
}));

vi.mock('@/integrations/supabase/client', () => ({
  supabase: {
    auth: {
      getSession: getSessionMock,
    },
    functions: {
      invoke: invokeMock,
    },
  },
}));

vi.mock('@/hooks/useHubStudents', () => ({
describe('DeskPeopleSurface', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.setSystemTime(new Date('2026-06-18T13:00:00.000Z'));
    invokeMock.mockClear();
    getSessionMock.mockResolvedValue({ data: { session: { user: { id: 'admin-1' } } } });
    invokeMock.mockImplementation((_name, options) => {
      if (options?.headers?.['x-mode'] === 'analytics') {
        return Promise.resolve({
          data: {
            total_users: 2,
            active_today: 2,
            active_week: 2,
            admin_count: 1,
            feature_adoption: {},
            total_attempts: 0,
            week_attempts: 0,
          },
          error: null,
        });
      }
      if (options?.method === 'POST') {
        const { class_id, field, value } = options.body as Record<string, unknown>;
        return Promise.resolve({
          data: { success: true, profile: { class_id, [field as string]: value } },
          error: null,
        });
      }
      return Promise.resolve({ data: users, error: null });
    });
  });

  it('combines student context with admin access management in one People workspace', async () => {
    renderPeople();

    // The room title lives in the workspace shell now, so the surface itself
    // carries only its landmark and the roster count.
    expect(await screen.findByRole('region', { name: 'Students' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/2 people/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Meghan Mullins')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('3/4')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Unread message')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Online on LSAT U')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Online')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Last active 2m ago')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Joshua')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Primary admin')).toBeInTheDocument();

    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Admins' }));

    expect(screen.queryByText('Meghan Mullins')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Joshua')).toBeInTheDocument();

    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Expand Joshua/i }));

    // The simplified People page only manages Drill access plus role. No
    // broad permission matrix, templates, or bulk grant/revoke.
    expect(screen.queryByText('Templates')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Role Management')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Protected primary admin')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows one whole-account access control per student', async () => {
    renderPeople();

    const row = await screen.findByTestId('people-row-student-1');

    expect(within(row).getByText(/Drill on/i)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(row).getByRole('button', { name: /Revoke All Access/i })).toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(screen.queryByRole('switch', { name: 'Bootcamps' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('switch', { name: 'Classroom' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('switch', { name: 'Practice' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('revokes every feature except the classroom home page', async () => {
    vi.spyOn(window, 'confirm').mockReturnValue(true);
    renderPeople();

    const row = await screen.findByTestId('people-row-student-1');
    fireEvent.click(within(row).getByRole('button', { name: /Revoke All Access/i }));

    const call = invokeMock.mock.calls.find(([, options]) => options?.body?.action === 'apply_template');
    expect(call).toBeTruthy();

    expect(call![1].body.class_id).toBe('student-1');
    expect(flags.has_classroom_access).toBe(false);
    expect(flags.has_drill_access).toBe(false);
    expect(flags.has_chat_access).toBe(false);
    expect(flags.has_practice_access).toBe(false);
  });

    renderPeople();

    await screen.findByTestId('people-row-student-1');
    // The point of archiving: the list shows who you are actually teaching.

    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Archived' }));

    expect(await screen.findByTestId('people-row-student-2')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByTestId('people-row-student-1')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('archives a student through the edge function', async () => {
    vi.spyOn(window, 'confirm').mockReturnValue(true);
    renderPeople();

    const row = await screen.findByTestId('people-row-student-1');
    fireEvent.click(within(row).getByRole('button', { name: /Meghan Mullins/i }));
    fireEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: /^Archive$/i }));

    const call = invokeMock.mock.calls.find(([, options]) => options?.body?.action === 'archive_student');
    expect(call).toBeTruthy();
    expect(call![1].body.class_id).toBe('student-1');
  });

  it('refuses to delete unless the email is typed exactly', async () => {
    const promptSpy = vi.spyOn(window, 'prompt').mockReturnValue('wrong@example.com');
    renderPeople();

    const row = await screen.findByTestId('people-row-student-1');
    fireEvent.click(within(row).getByRole('button', { name: /Meghan Mullins/i }));
    fireEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: /Delete permanently/i }));

ANY(\"has_practice_access\"),\n    \"/sets (Saved sets)\": ANY(\"has_drill_access\"),\n    \"/classroom/ta (TA worksheets)\": ANY(\"has_ta_access\"),\n    \"/journal (Wrong answers)\": \"has_flagged_access\", \"has_analytics_access\", \"has_export_access\",\n          \"has_classroom_access\", \"has_chat_access\", \"has_schedule_access\", \"has_ta_access\"]\nNON_FLOOR_EXCEPT_TA = [\"has_drill_access\", \"has_practice_access\", \"has_activity_access\", \"has_bootcamp_access\",\n                       \"has_journal_access\", \"has_flagged_access\", \"has_analytics_access\", \"has_export_access\"]\n\n\ndef migrate(f):\n    f = dict(f)\n    # stage 1\n    f[\"has_practice_access\"] = f[\"has_drill_access\"]\n    f[\"has_ta_access\"] = f[\"has_classroom_access\"]\n    f[\"has_journal_access\"] = f[\"has_journal_access\"] or f[\"has_waj_access\"]\n    # stage 3\n    if all(f[k] for k in TWELVE):\n        tier = \"tier_1\"\n    elif f[\"has_classroom_access\"] and not any(f[k] for k in NON_FLOOR_EXCEPT_TA):\n        tier = \"tier_3\"\n        f[\"has_chat_access\"] = True\n        f[\"has_schedule_access\"] = True\n        f[\"has_ta_access\"] = False\n    else:\n        tier = \"tier_2\"\n    return f, tier\n\n\ntiers = {}\nchanges = []\nfor name, before in rows:\n    after, tier = migrate(before)\n    tiers.setdefault(tier, []).append(name)\n    for Each gate\n// falls back to ProtectedRoute on this flag, so for this test it counts as one.\nconst ASSIGNMENT_GATE_FLAGS: Record<string, string> = {\n  DrillAssignmentGate: 'has_drill_access',\n  TestingCentreGate: 'has_practice_access',\n  PracticeTestRunGate: 'has_practice_access',\n  ActivityAssignmentGate: 'has_activity_access',\n  TAAssignmentGate: 'has_ta_access',\n};\n\nfunction guardText(element: string): string {\n  let text = element;\n  for (const [gate, flag] of Object.entries(ASSIGNMENT_GATE_FLAGS)) {\n    if (element.includes(`<${gate}>`)) text += ` ProtectedRoute ${flag}`;\n  }\n  return text;\n}\n\ndescribe('rail catalog agrees with the route table', () => {\"\"\"\nassert s.count(old) == 1\ns = s.replace(old, new)\n\nold = \"\"\"      const element = routeElement(ROUTE_FOR[item.id]);\n\n      // A padlock over an unguarded route is the /bootcamps bug.\"\"\"\nnew = \"\"\"      const element = guardText(routeElement(ROUTE_FOR[item.id]));\n\n      // A padlock over an unguarded route is the /bootcamps bug.\"\"\"\nassert s.count(old) == 1\ns = s.replace(old, new)\n\nold = \"\"\"    expect(routeElement('/journal')).toContain('has_journal_access');\n    expect(routeElement('/journal')).toContain('has_waj_access');\n\"\"\"\nnew = \"\"\"    expect(routeElement('/journal')).toContain('has_journal_access');\n    // The Wrong answer journal switch is retired: one switch, one page.\n    expect(routeElement('/journal')).not.toContain('has_waj_access');\n\"\"\"\nassert s.count(old) == 1\ns = s.replace(old, new)\nopen(p, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(s)\nprint('ok')\n"}

const renderPage = () =>
  render(
    <MemoryRouter>
      <TestingCentre />
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );

/**
 * The PrepTest field is a searchable combobox: a popover over a command list,
 * opened by clicking its trigger. Queried by accessible name, which is the
 * point of the aria-labelledby on it — <label for> does not name a <button>,
 * so before that this control announced its value and never its field.
 */
const openPrepTests = () =>
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /Take a section/ }));
    expect(screen.getByTestId("section-selector")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("offers a PrepTest whose RC section is short, and says so", async () => {
    renderPage();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /Take a practice test/ }));

    await waitFor(() => screen.getByRole("combobox", { name: /PREPTEST/i }));
    openPrepTests();

    // Both PrepTests are offered. Under the old rule PT 130 was hidden.
    const short = await screen.findByRole("option", { name: /PrepTest 130/ });
    expect(screen.getByRole("option", { name: /PrepTest 131/ })).toBeTruthy();

    fireEvent.click(short);

Build notes (2026-09-22)\n\nCorrections found while building, against sections 3 and 5:\n\n- **Defaults were already locked.** Live `profiles` defaults are Messages and Schedule on, everything else off, Classroom included. Section 3 said all true. Stage 3 therefore only moves the Classroom default to on.\n- **Tier 3 drift answered by default.** Open item 1 took the consistent answer: any switch away from the Tier 3 floor relabels the student Tier 2, same as Tier 1. Enforced by `profiles_access_tier_drift()`.\n- **Saved Sets moves for four students, not two.** Meghan Mullins and priyanka lose `/sets` (no Drill, so it was a dead end). Sandra and Ivory Johnson gain it (Drill on, Practice off). Verified with a per-student reachability diff over the 41-row snapshot.\n- **The 14 Tier 3 students lose the TA-worksheets switch** (it followed Classroom). Their own assigned worksheets still open through `TAAssignmentGate`, so no reachable worksheet is lost.\n- **Stage 6 is smaller than planned.** `/section`, `/classroom/:id`, `/classroom/item/:id` and `/guided/*` already open on Classroom, which Tier 3 keeps. Gated per item: `/activities/:assignmentId`, `/classroom/ta/:assignmentId`, `/drill`, `/rc*`, `/testing`, `/testing/test/:runId`.\n- **Practice-test sittings live in the browser**, not in `practice_test_runs`, so the route gate is the real enforcement; the new insert policy is a backstop.\n- **Archive and reset clear the tier** to undecided, so a returning student lands in the signup queue.\n'''\nopen(p,'w',encoding='utf-8').write(s+'\\n')\nEOF\ngit add -A && git commit -q -F - <<'EOF'\nStudent access tiers: three templates over the twelve switches\n\nA tier is a template that sets the switches; the switches stay what the app\nenforces. Tier 1 = all twelve on. Tier 2 = hand-picked. Tier 3 = Classroom,\nMessages and Schedule plus assigned items only. Access never changes by itself.\n\nStage 1: every switch now gates what its label says. Testing Centre follows\nPractice tests, Saved sets follow Drill, TA worksheets follow their own switch,\nWrong answers reads one switch (has_waj_access retired). Migration copies each\nstudent's effective access into the column that now guards it.\n\nStage 2: profiles.access_tier, admin-guarded, with a drift trigger that\nrelabels Tier 1 or Tier 3 as Tier 2 when a switch leaves the template.\nadmin-manage-users gains set_tier.\n\nStage 3: existing accounts tiered by current switches (21 / 6 / 14). The 14\nclassroom-only students become Tier 3 and regain Messages and Schedule. New\nsignups default to the floor, undecided.\n\nStage 4: a Stripe payment no longer grants access. It notifies the admins.\n\nStage 5: signup queue on Today and Students. Checkmark picks a tier; X puts\nthe student on Tier 3.\n\nStage 6: AssignmentGate. An assignment opens its own runner (activity, TA\nworksheet, homework or guided drill run, bootcamp day, PrepTest leg) without\nopening the feature.\n\nStage 7: practice_test_assignments. Assigning a PrepTest opens that one full\nsitting; the Testing Centre lists only assigned PrepTests without the switch.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\ngit log --oneline -1", "description": "Update plan notes and commit"}

{"command": "git push -q -u origin feat/student-access-tiers 2>&1 | tail -3; gh pr create --base main --head feat/student-access-tiers --title \"Student access tiers (all seven stages)\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nImplements `plans/student-access-tiers.md`, approved 2026-09-22. A tier is a template that sets the twelve switches; the switches stay what the app enforces. Access never changes by itself.\n\n## What changes\n- **Stage 1: switches mean their labels.** Testing Centre follows Practice tests, Saved sets follow Drill, TA worksheets have their own switch, Wrong answers reads one switch (`has_waj_access` retired). The migration copies effective access into the new guarding column.\n- **Stage 2: `profiles.access_tier`**, admin-guarded, plus a drift trigger (Tier 1 or Tier 3 relabels to Tier 2 when a switch leaves the template) and a `set_tier` action.\n- **Stage 3: tier every account.** 21 go to Tier 1, 6 to Tier 2 and 14 to Tier 3. The 14 regain Messages and Schedule. New signups default to the floor, with no tier yet.\n- **Stage 4: payment no longer grants access.** It sends the admins a `payment_needs_tier` notification instead.\n- **Stage 5: signup queue** on Today and Students. The checkmark picks a tier; the X means Tier 3.\n- **Stage 6: `AssignmentGate`.** An assignment opens its own runner (activity, TA worksheet, homework or guided drill, bootcamp day, PrepTest leg) without opening the whole feature.\n- **Stage 7: `practice_test_assignments`,** plus an assign control on each student's panel. The Testing Centre lists only assigned PrepTests for students without the switch.\n\n## Per-student effect (diffed over the 41-row snapshot of 2026-09-22)\n- The 14 classroom-only students gain Messages and Schedule (deliberate, decision 17).\n- The same 14 lose the TA-worksheet *switch*, but their assigned worksheets still open through the gate.\n- Saved sets: Meghan Mullins and priyanka lose them (no Drill, so they were a dead end). - **Tiers correct themselves.** If you turn one switch off for a Tier 1 student, they're labelled Tier 2 automatically. I answered your open question the same way for Tier 3: turning on one extra switch makes them Tier 2.

Tool result:
# Context Map

The repo has more than one bounded context, each with its own language. This map points to where each glossary lives.

## Contexts

- [Public surface](./src/pages/CONTEXT.md) — the unauthenticated landing + sign-in page (`Auth.tsx`): how LSAT U describes itself to a prospective student.
- [Teacher workspace](./src/pages/teacher/CONTEXT.md) — Joshua's admin operator surface (`/teacher`): assigning prepared material to students and the transcript pipeline.
- [Teacher Desk page](./src/pages/teacher/desk/CONTEXT.md) — the named visual regions of the desk page itself (the boxes on screen), nested under the teacher workspace. Names the layout so we can revise it precisely.
- [Guided Drills & Explanations](./src/components/drill/CONTEXT.md) — the practice domain: guided drills, the question bank, drill instruction cards, and per-question explanations (the Phase 2 authoring work).
- [Classroom](./src/components/classroom/CONTEXT.md) — the student's own room: the Classroom frame, its sections, and the flag-versus-wrong-answer distinction.
- [Reading Comprehension](./src/features/rc/CONTEXT.md) — full-passage RC drilling: passages, sessions, attempts, reveal modes, status, and the adaptive picker.
- [Activity Centre](./src/features/activity-centre/CONTEXT.md) — coach-led skill exercises a student is assigned, and the Read, Recall, Colour drill that runs inside one: Chunk, Detail, Coverage, The Recall, Report Card.
- [Booking](./src/components/booking/CONTEXT.md) — paying for and scheduling tutoring time: Packages, Booking Passes, the public microsite, and the Single Session (Slots, Holds, Bookers).

## Relationships

- Both surfaces describe the same product but to different audiences (prospective student vs. operator), so a word can carry different weight in each. Check the right glossary before reusing a term.
- A **flag** (Classroom) and a **wrong answer** (Wrong Answer Journal) are different things on different tables: a flag is chosen mid-question, a wrong answer is earned by missing one. Never collapse the two, and never let "flagged" drift into meaning "struggled with".
- **Set** now means one thing everywhere: the group a student assembles and drills. It used to mean a passage's position within its section in Reading Comprehension; that sense is now called **Position** (the data still carries `setNumber`). The collision was retired when the LR and RC pickers merged into one surface — a primary noun that changed meaning when you flipped the mode was the confusion the merge existed to remove. See `docs/adr/0006-rc-selection-is-question-grain.md`.
