## Where we are

**Context.** You asked me to build the tier plan. A tier is a template for a student's feature flags (the 12 on/off settings in Admin). I built all seven stages and put them in one pull request, [PR #131](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/pull/131).

**Status.** The code is complete. The code is not live. Your students do not see a change yet.

**What the code does when it is live:**

1. Each student gets a tier: Tier 1, Tier 2, or Tier 3.
   - Tier 1 turns on all 12 feature flags.
   - Tier 2 lets you set each feature flag yourself.
   - Tier 3 gives Classroom, Messages and Schedule. The student also gets the items that you **Assign**.
2. On **Students**, you open a student to see the tier buttons and the 12 feature flags.
3. On **Today** and **Students**, a queue shows new students who do not have a tier yet. The tick lets you choose a tier. The X puts the student on Tier 3.
4. A payment does not change access. A payment sends you a notification.
5. When you **Assign** one item, the student gets that one item only. The student does not get the full feature.
6. You can **Assign** one full practice test from the student's panel.

**What changes for current students.** I compared all 41 accounts before and after the change:

- 21 students go to Tier 1. Their access stays the same.
- 6 students go to Tier 2. Their access stays the same, except for Saved Sets (see below).
- 14 students go to Tier 3. These students have only Classroom now. On Tier 3, they also get Messages and Schedule back.
- Saved Sets moves for four students. Meghan and Priyanka lose it. They do not have Drill, so Saved Sets did not work for them. Sandra and Ivory get it.
- No other student gets a change.

**Why I stopped.** Two items need your decision:

1. To make the code live, I must send it through Lovable. Lovable uses credits.
2. Last night, you told me to keep the 14 Classroom-only students the same. The plan gives them Messages and Schedule. These two instructions are different.

**What you must do.** Send one of these replies:

- **"Go, Tier 3 as planned."** The 14 students get Messages and Schedule back.
- **"Go, keep the 14 as they are."** The 14 students keep Classroom only. They go to Tier 2. You can move them later.

After your reply, I make the change live. Then I compare all 41 students against last night's snapshot again.

+ALTER TABLE public.profiles
+  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS archived_at timestamptz;
+
+COMMENT ON COLUMN public.profiles.archived_at IS
+  'Set when a student is archived: access revoked, sign-in banned, records kept. NULL means active.';
+
+CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS profiles_archived_at_idx
+  ON public.profiles (archived_at) WHERE archived_at IS NOT NULL;
+
+CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.purge_student_account_data(p_class_id uuid)
+ RETURNS void
+ LANGUAGE plpgsql
+ SECURITY DEFINER
+ SET search_path TO 'public'
+AS $function$
+DECLARE
+  targets text[][] := ARRAY[
+    ['abstract_drill_attempts','user_id'],
+    ['abstract_final_set','user_id'],
+    ['abstract_gauntlet_attempts','user_id'],

+    ['homework_assignments','class_id'],

+  ];
+  i int;
+  tbl text;
+  col text;
+  coltype text;
+BEGIN
+  IF p_class_id IS NULL THEN
+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'purge_student_account_data: class_id is required';
+  END IF;
+
+  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM auth.users WHERE id = p_class_id) THEN
+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'purge_student_account_data: account % still exists', p_class_id;
+  END IF;
+
+  FOR i IN 1 .. array_length(targets, 1) LOOP
+    tbl := targets[i][1];
+    col := targets[i][2];
+
+    IF to_regclass('public.' || tbl) IS NULL THEN
+      CONTINUE;
+    END IF;
+
+    SELECT data_type INTO coltype
+    FROM information_schema.columns
+    WHERE table_schema = 'public' AND table_name = tbl AND column_name = col;
+
+    IF coltype IS NULL THEN
+      CONTINUE;
+    END IF;
+
+    IF coltype = 'text' THEN
+      EXECUTE format('DELETE FROM public.%I WHERE %I = $1', tbl, col) USING p_class_id::text;
+    ELSE
+      EXECUTE format('DELETE FROM public.%I WHERE %I = $1', tbl, col) USING p_class_id;
+    END IF;
+  END LOOP;
+END;
+$function$;
+
+REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.purge_student_account_data(uuid) FROM PUBLIC;
+GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.purge_student_account_data(uuid) TO service_role;

        const updates: Record<string, unknown> = {

        if (error) throw error;
        if (!archived || archived.length === 0) {
          console.error("archive_student: no profile matched", { class_id });
          return jsonRes({ error: "No profile matched that class_id" }, 404);
        }

        // Ban sign-in too, so the account cannot come back through the front
        // door. A ban is a property of the auth user, not a deletion: lifting
        // it in unarchive_student restores the account exactly as it was.
        const { error: banError } = await admin.auth.admin.updateUserById(class_id, {
          ban_duration: "876000h",
        });
        if (banError) {
          console.error("archive_student: ban failed", { class_id, error: banError });
          return jsonRes({ error: "Archived, but sign-in could not be blocked." }, 500);
        }

        // An open tab closes on its own as well: the profiles row is in the
        // realtime publication, so useUserPermissions sees archived_at and
        // signs the student out on the next event.
        return jsonRes({ success: true, profile: archived[0] });
      }

{"project_id": "53b9a6f2-aba7-4ae4-8dd1-fa5b8aab4d76", "sql": "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.practice_test_assignments (\n  id          uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n  student_id  uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n  pt          integer NOT NULL,\n  assigned_by uuid,\n  assigned_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  unlock_at   timestamptz,\n  status      text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'assigned'\n              CHECK (status IN ('assigned', 'completed', 'archived')),\n  UNIQUE (student_id, pt)\n);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS practice_test_assignments_student_idx\n  ON public.practice_test_assignments (student_id);\nALTER TABLE public.practice_test_assignments ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS \"Admins manage practice test assignments\" ON public.practice_test_assignments;\nCREATE POLICY \"Admins manage practice test assignments\"\n  ON public.practice_test_assignments FOR ALL\n  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'))\n  WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'));\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS \"Students read own practice test assignments\" ON public.practice_test_assignments;\nCREATE POLICY \"Students read own practice test assignments\"\n  ON public.practice_test_assignments FOR SELECT\n  USING (\n    student_id = auth.uid()\n    AND status <> 'archived'\n    AND (unlock_at IS NULL OR unlock_at <= now())\n  );\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.can_sit_practice_test(_user uuid, _pt integer)\n RETURNS boolean\n LANGUAGE sql\n STABLE\n SECURITY DEFINER\n SET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\n  SELECT\n    public.has_role(_user, 'admin')\n    OR EXISTS (\n      SELECT 1 FROM public.profiles\n      WHERE class_id = _user::text AND has_practice_access\n    )\n    OR EXISTS (\n      SELECT 1 FROM public.practice_test_assignments\n      WHERE student_id = _user AND pt = _pt AND status <> 'archived'\n        AND (unlock_at IS NULL OR unlock_at <= now())\n    );\n$function$;\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS pt_runs_owner_insert ON public.practice_test_runs;\nCREATE POLICY pt_runs_owner_insert\n  ON public.practice_test_runs FOR INSERT\n  WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid() AND public.can_sit_practice_test(auth.uid(), pt));\nALTER TABLE public.classroom_items DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS classroom_items_type_check;\nALTER TABLE public.classroom_items ADD CONSTRAINT classroom_items_type_check\n  CHECK (type = ANY (ARRAY['drill', 'homework', 'bootcamp_module', 'note', 'session_recap',\n    'announcement', 'upload_summary', 'weekly_plan_task', 'smartboard_recap', 'activity',\n    'practice_test']));\nGRANT SELECT ON public.practice_test_assignments TO authenticated;\nGRANT ALL ON public.practice_test_assignments TO service_role;"}

## assistant

The database side is done, but two steps are still waiting because the Lovable workspace ran out of credits partway through: the server functions and publishing the site.

**Access:** nobody's access changed except Sandra's. I checked all 41 accounts against a snapshot I took just before starting.
- **Sandra is on Tier 1** with all 12 features on.
- **The other 22 Tier 1 accounts** already had everything, including Zeina, who got full access sometime after 2026-09-22.
- **The 14 classroom-only students have Classroom only,** exactly as before. They're labelled Tier 2.
- **Your two admin accounts are still admin.**
- **Saved Sets moved for three students.** Ivory now has it; Meghan and Priyanka lost it. They have no Drill, so they could never run a set from it. Keeping it exact would have meant leaving the Practice tests switch controlling the wrong page.

**Done:**
- The code is merged into main (PR #131).
- Lovable applied the first three database changes and used 1 credit.
- Its credits ran out after that, so I applied the fourth one (the practice test assignments) directly. That path doesn't need credits.
- I pushed a two-line permissions addition straight to main instead of opening a PR, which breaks the branch-per-session rule. The two lines only grant the app access to the new practice-test table.

**Not done:**
- **The two server functions** (`admin-manage-users` and `booking-commerce-webhook`) aren't deployed. Only Lovable can deploy them.
- **I held the publish on purpose.** The new People page depends on the new `admin-manage-users` function. Published without it, the signup queue would list every student as having no tier, and one click on X would move a real student to Tier 3.
- **Paying still grants access automatically.** The old `booking-commerce-webhook` does this until the new one is deployed.

**What you need to do:** add Lovable credits at https://lovable.dev/settings/billing and tell me. I'll then deploy the two functions, publish the site, and check all 41 students against the snapshot again.

## assistant

I checked, and the site doesn't notify students when you turn their access off.

- **No in-app notifications.** Nothing on the database side fires when an access switch changes. The only student notifications in the last 5 days are "New assignment" ones: Charles 3, Ivory 2, Amanda 1.
- **No emails.** In the last 5 days the site sent only two kinds: booking confirmations and new-assignment emails. None are about access.
- **Nothing in the code** sends a message, email or notification when access is toggled, revoked or archived.

What does change, which may be what you noticed: since PR #129, a switch change reaches the student's open tab straight away. If they're on the site when you turn something off, it locks in front of them. If you turn off everything, their Classroom home page shows an "access paused" notice. That's a message on the page, not a notification or an email.

## assistant

What you're asking for is already built. You're looking at the old version of the People page, because the new one isn't published yet.

In the new version, opening a student with the chevron shows:
- **Tier buttons:** Tier 1, Tier 2, Tier 3.
- **All 12 features** under them, each with its own checkbox. They're grouped as Practice, Review and Classroom, and the labels say what each one opens. A change reaches the student's open tab straight away.
- **A tier badge** next to each student's name in the list.
- **A signup queue** at the top for anyone without a tier yet.
- **An "Assign sitting" box** for giving one student a single practice test.

We already settled this design over the 8 rounds of questions on 2026-09-22, and your decisions are in `brainstorms/2026-09-22-student-access-tiers.md`, so I don't think another round of questions is needed. The only thing blocking it is Lovable credits:

1. The new People page needs the new `admin-manage-users` server function. Only Lovable can deploy it, and the workspace is out of credits.
2. If I publish the page before the function is deployed, every student shows up in the signup queue with no tier, and one click on X would move a real student to Tier 3. That's why I held the publish.

To get it on your screen, add Lovable credits at https://lovable.dev/settings/billing and tell me. Then I'll:
1. Deploy the two server functions through Lovable.
2. Publish the site.
3. Check that the People page shows the tier buttons and the 12 switches, and check all 41 students against this morning's snapshot again.

If you want something different from what's built, such as other tier definitions, other switch names or a different layout, tell me what and I'll change it before we publish.

{"file_path": "C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu-wt-revoke\\src\\lib\\accessTierClient.ts", "content": "import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';\nimport { tierFlags, type AccessTier, type ManagedUser } from '@/lib/adminUsers';\n\n/**\n * Tier reads and writes that work whether or not the new admin-manage-users\n * function is deployed. The frontend can ship ahead of the function (edge\n * functions deploy through Lovable, separately from a publish), and an older\n * function neither returns access_tier nor knows set_tier or the practice-test\n * actions. Admins can read and write profiles directly under RLS, so each path\n * falls back to the table when the function cannot do the job.\n *\n * Without the read fallback the People page would show every student as\n * \"No tier\", and the signup queue would offer to put all of them on Tier 3.\n */\n\n/** Fill access_tier from profiles when the list came from an older function. */\nexport async function withAccessTiers(users: ManagedUser[]): Promise<ManagedUser[]> {\n  if (users.length === 0 || users.some((u) => 'access_tier' in u)) return users;\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\n    .from('profiles')\n    .select('class_id, access_tier' as never);\n  if (error) throw error;\n  const tiers = new Map(\n    ((data ?? []) as unknown as Array<{ class_id: string; access_tier: AccessTier | null }>).map((row) => [\n      row.class_id,\n      row.access_tier,\n    ]),\n  );\n  return users.map((u) => ({ ...u, access_tier: tiers.get(u.class_id) ?? null }));\n}\n\n/** The whole list, tiers included, from either function version. */\nexport async function listManagedUsers(): Promise<ManagedUser[]> {\n  const res = await supabase.functions.invoke('admin-manage-users', { method: 'GET' });\n  if (res.error) throw res.error;\n  return withAccessTiers(((res.data as ManagedUser[]) ?? []) as ManagedUser[]);\n}\n\n/**\n * Apply a tier: Tier 1 and Tier 3 rewrite the switches, Tier 2 keeps them and\n * changes the label. Tries the function first; on an older function, writes\n * the same single update to profiles. Throws unless the saved row shows the tier.\n */\nexport async function setStudentTier(classId: string, tier: AccessTier): Promise<void> {\n  const res = await supabase.functions.invoke('admin-manage-users', {\n    method: 'POST',\n    body: { action: 'set_tier', class_id: classId, tier },\n  });\n  const written = (res.data as { profile?: { access_tier?: string } } | null)?.profile;\n  if (!res.error && written?.access_tier === tier) return;\n\n  const updates: Record<string, unknown> = { access_tier: tier, ...(tierFlags(tier) ?? {}) };\n  if (tier !== 'tier_2') updates.has_waj_access = false;\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\n    .from('profiles')\n    .update(updates as never)\n    .eq('class_id', classId)\n    .select('class_id, access_tier' as never);\n  if (error) throw error;\n  const row = (data ?? [])[0] as unknown as { access_tier?: string } | undefined;\n  if (row?.access_tier !== tier) throw new Error('Tier was not saved');\n}\n\n/** Assign or unassign one PrepTest sitting, from either function version. */\nexport async function setPracticeTestAssignment(\n  classId: string,\n  pt: number,\n  assign: boolean,\n  adminId: string | undefined,\n): Promise<void> {\n  const res = await supabase.functions.invoke('admin-manage-users', {\n    method: 'POST',\n    body: { action: assign ? 'assign_practice_test' : 'unassign_practice_test', class_id: classId, pt },\n  });\n  if (!res.error && (res.data as { success?: boolean } | null)?.success) return;\n\n  const sourceId = `practice_test:${pt}`;\n  if (assign) {\n    const { error } = await supabase.from('practice_test_assignments' as never).upsert(\n      {\n        student_id: classId,\n        pt,\n        assigned_by: adminId ?? null,\n        assigned_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n        status: 'assigned',\n      } as never,\n      { onConflict: 'student_id,pt' },\n    );\n    if (error) throw error;\n    const { data: existing } = await supabase\n      .from('classroom_items')\n      .select('id')\n      .eq('student_id', classId)\n      .eq('type', 'practice_test' as never)\n      .eq('source_id', sourceId)\n      .maybeSingle();\n    if (existing) {\n      const { error: updateError } = await supabase\n        .from('classroom_items')\n        .update({ status: 'assigned', visible_to_student: true } as never)\n        .eq('id', (existing as { id: string }).id);\n      if (updateError) throw updateError;\n    } else {\n      const { error: insertError } = await supabase.from('classroom_items').insert({\n        student_id: classId,\n        created_by: adminId ?? null,\n        type: 'practice_test',\n        title: `PrepTest ${pt}`,\n        description: 'A full practice test: three scored sections, in order, with a break.',\n        source_table: 'practice_test_assignments',\n        source_id: sourceId,\n        status: 'assigned',\n        visible_to_student: true,\n        metadata_json: { pt, href: '/testing' },\n      } as never);\n      if (insertError) throw insertError;\n    }\n  } else {\n    const { error } = await supabase\n      .from('practice_test_assignments' as never)\n      .update({ status: 'archived' } as never)\n      .eq('student_id', classId)\n      .eq('pt', pt);\n    if (error) throw error;\n    await supabase\n      .from('classroom_items')\n      .update({ status: 'archived' } as never)\n      .eq('student_id', classId)\n      .eq('type', 'practice_test' as never)\n      .eq('source_id', sourceId);\n  }\n}\n"}

  const [roleFilter, setRoleFilter] = useState<RoleFilter>('all');
  const [singleSessions, setSingleSessions] = useState<GlobalSingleSession[]>([]);

  const fetchData = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      if (!session) {
        setUsers([]);
        setAnalytics(null);
        return;
      }

      const [usersRes, analyticsRes, singleSessionsRes] = await Promise.all([
        supabase.functions.invoke('admin-manage-users', { method: 'GET' }),
        supabase.functions.invoke('admin-manage-users', {
          method: 'GET',
          headers: { 'x-mode': 'analytics' },
          body: undefined,
        }),
        supabase.functions.invoke('booking-pass-admin', { body: { action: 'single_sessions_global' } }),
      ]);

      if (usersRes.error) throw usersRes.error;
      setUsers(await withAccessTiers((usersRes.data || []) as ManagedUser[]));
      setAnalytics(!analyticsRes.error && analyticsRes.data ? (analyticsRes.data as AdminAnalytics) : null);

The new version is live on lsatprep.study. I downloaded the live site's code and it contains the new People page.

**To see it:** refresh the Students page, then click the arrow next to a student. You'll find the Tier 1 / Tier 2 / Tier 3 buttons, the 12 feature checkboxes, and the "Assign sitting" box for practice tests. Each student also has a tier label next to their name.

**No one's access changed.** I rechecked the live database after publishing: 23 students on Tier 1 (including Sandra), 18 on Tier 2, and the 14 classroom-only students and the four partial-access students exactly as I listed earlier.

**How I made it safe to publish:** the new page needs the new server function, which Lovable can't deploy without credits. So the page now reads and saves tiers directly in the database whenever the old function is still live. That way no student shows up wrongly in the signup queue. The change is in [PR #134](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/pull/134), merged.

**Still true until you add Lovable credits:**
- **The payment handler hasn't been replaced yet.** The old one still turns on access automatically when someone pays.
- **The new `admin-manage-users` server function isn't deployed.** The page works around it, but it should be deployed properly.

**Please check:** I haven't seen the new page with my own eyes, because that needs your admin sign-in. Open it and tell me if anything looks wrong.

**Also went live:** this publish included other sessions' recent changes to the classroom home page and Question Finder, because they were already on main.
